- impact: The owner opens his job and cannot see which salaries were counted for it, whether this month's salary arrived, or how much is missing. With salary day 28 and a fiscal month starting on 28, this is the page he would use to check the month, and it is empty.
- fix: Failing test first: memory repos with one employer (payer key K, started 2026-01-01) and three deposits from K dated 2026-08-27, 2026-09-28, 2026-10-27 plus one unrelated deposit; a new use case 'SourceIncome.load(sourceId, payday)' must return the three rows grouped by fiscal month, with received sum per month and expected (or 'not available' when expectedMinor is null, rule 10). Then show that list and a 'this month: received X of expected Y' line on the detail page, each row opening the transaction. Unify the two creation forms so both ask for (optional) amount and day.
- second reviewer: IncomeSourceDetailScreen.kt:91-96 renders a fixed NotYetLine(INCSRC_TX_NOT_YET) and the facts card (lines 85-89) shows only payday, expected amount and payer-known, nothing received. attributedSourceId (IncomeSignals.kt:27-35) has a single production caller, the late-salary alert at IncomeSignals.kt:130, so no use case lists transactions per source; ManageEmployers.kt:69-71 creates a JOB with no expectedMinor/day while ManageIncomeSources.kt:84-91 stores both, confirming the two-form mismatch. The proposed SourceIncome use case would remove the symptom; note that attributedSourceId attributes any unconfirmed incoming from the payer key, not only salary-typed rows, so the test should state which is intended.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: في صفحة مصدر الدخل تحب تشوف إيه بالظبط: قايمة المرتبات شهر بشهر، ولا سطر واحد «وصل كام من المتوقع الشهر ده»، ولا الاتنين؟

### sec-android-1 [T8] - Screenshots are blocked in the whole app whenever app lock is on (T8) - no owner decision recorded
- severity: medium | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: S | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/MainActivity.kt:122`
- evidence: hideWhenLocked() (lines 122-127) reads container.appLock.isEnabled(); if true it adds WindowManager FLAG_SECURE to the single activity window and, on API 33+, calls setRecentsScreenshotEnabled(false); if false it clears both. It is called from onCreate (35), onResume (104) and onPause (109). There is one activity for the whole app, so the block covers every screen, not just sensitive ones, and it stays on after unlocking. It was added as PLAN-0.6.md:16-17 item ANDROID-3 (goal: hide balances from the recents thumbnail). OVERRIDES.md section 21 (lines 307-313, the lock decision) says nothing about screenshots, and a grep of OVERRIDES.md for FLAG_SECURE / screenshot wording returned no hits. ARCHITECTURE.md:1044 still states there is no FLAG_SECURE (stale).
- impact: The owner (and any tester with lock on) cannot take screenshots anywhere in the app, including to report bugs. The only workaround today is turning the lock off, which also removes the recents protection. The behaviour is undocumented as a decision.
- fix: Ask the owner and record it in OVERRIDES section 21. Options: (a) keep as is; (b) a separate setting 'allow screenshots' next to the lock switch; (c) on API 33+ use only setRecentsScreenshotEnabled(false) (hides recents thumbnail, screenshots still work) and keep FLAG_SECURE only while the lock screen is actually showing; below API 33 there is no recents-only option. Also fix ARCHITECTURE.md:1044.
- second reviewer: native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/MainActivity.kt:122-127 sets FLAG_SECURE on the only activity window whenever AppLock.isEnabled() (AppLock.kt:50, the plain setting, not the locked state) is true, so screenshots are blocked on every screen even after unlocking; this is the only FLAG_SECURE site in native-app, so it is the full root cause of T8 and narrowing the flag would remove it. OVERRIDES.md section 21 (307-313) has no screenshot decision, PLAN-0.6.md:16-17 scoped ANDROID-3 only to the recents thumbnail, and ARCHITECTURE.md:1044 is stale. Fix option (c) only works on API 33+ and minSdk is 24 (build.gradle.kts:28), so older phones still need an owner choice.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Screenshots with lock on: block everywhere (current) / separate switch / hide only the recents thumbnail and allow screenshots after unlock?

### todo-transfers-2 [T9] - Transfer pairing is only amount + 1 day; second equal transfer loses its leg (plus full list of failure modes)
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:122`
- evidence: ownLegAlreadyThere() (lines 122-128) returns true if the target wallet has ANY row with opposite direction, same amount and currency within 1 day. addLegs passes it stored rows plus ctx.lines plus ctx.extra (line 89), so the auto leg of a first transfer counts as 'already there' for a second one. Scenario: two transfers of 5,000 from A to B on the same or next day -> first creates leg +500,000 on B, second is skipped -> A -1,000,000, B +500,000, total short by 500,000. Not tested (OwnTransferLegEffectTest has no two-transfer case). Every way transfers can go wrong, from the code read: (a) incoming-only never debits the other wallet (todo-transfers-1); (b) two equal transfers within a day -> one leg (this finding); (c) mergeIncoming (lines 54-66) deletes the auto leg and marks as internal ANY incoming SMS of the same amount on that wallet within a day, with no check of kind or party (todo-transfers-6); (d) manual transfer merge swallows any same-amount SMS, including a purchase (todo-transfers-3); (e) manual bank-to-cash + ATM SMS credits cash twice (todo-transfers-4); (f) real 'to: NAME' messages without digits are never recognised as own account and the zone decision adds no leg (todo-transfers-5); (g) fee inside the SMS amount: leg created with the gross amount (todo-transfers-7); (h) a named party whose digits equal the owner's other wallet stays unclassified until the question is answered (OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt:46, deliberate, review S1) so the other wallet gets nothing meanwhile; (i) different currencies inside one country: addLegs requires w.currency == t.currency (line 86) and AddTransaction rejects it (AddTransaction.kt:85), only the cross-country pair flow exists; (j) statement (non-SMS) rows are untouched by this effect (line 37, by design in the file header); (k) undo of the other bank's batch after a merge does not bring the auto leg back (documented warning at line 33); (l) transfers to other people are never auto-classified, they wait for the loan/support question (TransferParties.kt:255-262), so they sit as unclassified until answered. Exclusion from income/spend itself is sound once the kind is INTERNAL_TRANSFER (EconomicKind.kt:108, Liquidity.INTERNAL).
- impact: Wallet balances and the total drift by whole transfer amounts in ordinary situations (repeated equal transfers, one-sided messages), which is exactly the 'transfers are still wrong' complaint.
- fix: Failing test first: record two OUT own-transfer lines of 50,000 on the same day (same batch and separate batches) and assert wallet B has two IN legs. Fix: pair one-to-one instead of 'any exists': count candidates on the target against transfers already claiming them (a taken-set like mergeIncoming uses), and store an explicit pair id on both legs instead of deriving pairing from amount+day each time.
- second reviewer: OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:122-128 returns true for ANY opposite-direction same-amount row on the target within one day, and line 89 feeds it stored rows plus ctx.extra, so the first transfer's '#own-leg' (only excluded by id for its own base at line 126) suppresses the leg of a second equal transfer; no taken-set exists here unlike mergeIncoming line 47. OwnTransferLegEffectTest.kt:65-119 has no two-transfer case; I verified only this core claim, not every item of the (a)-(l) list.

### todo-types-1 [T10] - T10: a type answer is written on one transaction only, so the next similar one is asked again
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/SetEconomicKind.kt:112`
- evidence: SetEconomicKind.setOne (lines 97-117) only does txns.update(transactionId, TransactionPatch(economicKind, economicKindConfirmed = true, ...)). No party, rule or merchant is written. It is the write path for the review queue (ui/.../home/ReviewQueueScreen.kt:81), ReviewHistory.kt:129, 'person' with a kind (TransferPartyAnswers.kt:105, TransferPartyCard.kt:206), 'company' (TransferPartyAnswers.kt:84) and 'dues kind' (TransferPartyAnswers.kt:155). IncomeAnswers.markIncome (IncomeAnswers.kt:94-101, doc line 31 'for this transfer only') behaves the same for extra work / sale / other type. New transactions get a type at record time from only three sources (RecordLines.kt:83-95): applyTransferVerdict (own account, LABEL), applyKnownPayerSalary (employer account), plus PersonRulesEffect. Everything else arrives UNCLASSIFIED and is counted as a pending question by IncomingAskSource.kt:23-29 and TransferAskSource.kt:43-45. Scenario: a 1,500.00 transfer comes from sender X on 2026-10-03; owner opens review and picks 'gift' -> that row is confirmed. On 2026-11-03 another 1,500.00 from X arrives by SMS -> prepareRecordedLines finds no decision for X -> UNCLASSIFIED -> same question. Flow table: category per shop = remembered for future only (MerchantMemory.kt:19); LABEL = remembered and applied to old and new (TransferParties.kt:265-270); person dues/auto = applied at record time (PersonRules.kt:30); employer account rule = applied only if the box was ticked (PayerKindAnswers.kt:131); company kind = stored, never applied (TransferParties.kt:263); outgoing to person = asked every time by decision 75-5 (TransferParties.kt:16). Tests: the ask-again behaviour is pinned as intended for companies (TransferPartyAnswersTest.kt:80); I found no test that asserts 'second transfer from same sender takes the answered type' for the review-queue path.
- impact: The owner answers the same question for the same sender every month, the 'needs confirmation' counter never drains, and incoming money stays outside income until answered (EstimatedKinds.kt:80-85), so monthly income and remaining-to-spend are understated in the meantime.
- fix: Write the failing test first: record deposit A from sender X, answer via SetEconomicKind.setOne / IncomeAnswers.markIncome with 'remember for this sender', record deposit B from X through prepareRecordedLines, assert B has the same kind confirmed. Then add one sender-level memory: store the chosen kind on TransferParty (the field 'kind' already exists) for incoming answers, apply it in applyTransferVerdict for COMPANY and for a new 'remembered kind' case, and offer 'apply to the earlier ones too' with a count preview like EmployerRetroApply. Keep confirmed rows untouched. The three sub-causes are listed as separate findings (todo-types-3, -4, -5, -6).
- second reviewer: SetEconomicKind.kt:112-116 writes only a TransactionPatch on one id and IncomeAnswers.kt:94-100/133-142 does the same; RecordLines.kt:83-95 has only the transfer-verdict and known-payer sources, and applyTransferVerdict (TransferParties.kt:263) returns COMPANY/DISMISSED untouched, so a second deposit from the same sender stays UNCLASSIFIED and IncomingAskSource.kt:23-28 counts it as pending. The root cause does produce the T10 symptom, but most of it is documented design rather than a bug: company 'each amount asked alone' (TransferParties.kt:22-23), outgoing-to-person asked every time (OVERRIDES §75-5) and 'do not make the answer a rule, add an option' (OVERRIDES.md:2439). The proposed auto-apply for COMPANY therefore reverses owner decisions and must go to the owner as an open question, not be decided by the implementer; the 'remember for this sender' checkbox variant is consistent with the §108 pattern.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: لما تختار نوع لتحويل داخل من حساب معين: تحب البرنامج يفتكر النوع ده لكل اللي جاي من نفس الحساب لوحده، ولا يسألك مرة واحدة «أفتكره للمرات الجاية؟»، ولا يفضل يسأل كل مرة زي دلوقتي؟ وهل يطبّقه كمان على التحويلات القديمة من نفس الحساب؟

### todo-transfers-1 [T11] - Incoming own-account transfer credits one wallet and never debits the other: 10,000 shows as extra money
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:72`
- evidence: addLegs() only selects lines with observedDirection == Direction.OUT (lines 70-74) before creating the automatic second leg. OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt:45-49 confirms an INCOMING line as INTERNAL_TRANSFER when the sender digits equal another wallet's last 4, but nothing then creates the OUT leg on that other wallet. Balances are driven purely by observedDirection (core/ZakatHawl.kt:21), not by kind. Step by step with 1,000,000 minor: wallet A (last4 1111, SMS linked) receives 'حوالة واردة / من حساب ••••2222 / 10000'; wallet B (2222) is the owner's other account whose bank sends no message. Row = IN 1,000,000 on A, confirmed internal transfer; B gets no row. A +1,000,000, B +0, so the 'with you now' total rises by 1,000,000 although no money entered. If the sender line is a NAME (golden/sms.json line 17: 'حوالة محلية واردة / من:TEST PERSON / إلى:1111') the party has no last4, the row stays UNCLASSIFIED IN: still +1,000,000 in the balance and listed as pending incoming (core/EstimatedKinds.kt:80-85). ManageTransfers.otherLegs (ManageTransfers.kt:183-190) does handle both directions, so the record-time effect and the zone decision disagree. No test: OwnTransferLegEffectTest has 7 tests, all start from an OUT line (recordOut) or an IN that follows one. Two other code paths give the same symptom and are reported separately: todo-transfers-4 (cash credited twice) and todo-transfers-7 (fee inside the amount).
- impact: The owner's total money is overstated by the full transfer amount until he fixes it by hand; the hawl/zakat balance series and 'understand your balance' inherit the same error.
- fix: Failing test first: in OwnTransferLegEffectTest record ONLY an incoming line on wallet A with body 'حوالة واردة\nمن حساب ••••2222\nمبلغ:SAR 10000' through OwnAccountByLast4Effect + OwnTransferLegEffect and assert LoadWithYouNow total is unchanged and wallet B has an OUT leg of 1,000,000. Fix: make addLegs symmetric (confirmed internal IN line -> ownLegOn(t, otherWallet) as OUT, using the from-side digits), reuse ownLegAlreadyThere, and let mergeIncoming keep handling the later real message. Needs the owner decision below because OVERRIDES §112-3 only decided the outgoing direction.
- second reviewer: OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:70-74 filters addLegs to Direction.OUT only, while OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt:45-49 confirms a digits-only incoming line (shape exists: golden/sms.json:23 'حوالة واردة / من حساب ••••7519') as internal transfer; core/ZakatHawl.kt:21 adds it as +amount on the receiving wallet and nothing debits the other, whereas ManageTransfers.kt:187-189 does build the OUT leg for the same case. OVERRIDES.md:2566 (§112-3) only speaks of 'معرّف الصادر', so the symmetric fix needs an owner decision; I could not find the T11 text in the repo, so the link to the owner's exact 10,000 symptom is plausible but not proven.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: لما توصل رسالة إن فلوس دخلت حسابك من حسابك التاني، والبنك التاني ما بعتش رسالة خصم: البرنامج يخصم المبلغ من الحساب التاني لوحده (زي ما بيعمل في الاتجاه العكسي)، ولا يسألك الأول؟

### todo-transfers-3 [T12] - Manual-transfer merge matches any SMS of the same amount, and its tests never use a real message or the real effect chain
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/OwnTransferManualMerge.kt:35`
- evidence: legOfTransfer() (lines 35-41) accepts a row when: no transferToWalletId, same amount and currency, kind not confirmed as something else, within 1 day, and (wallet == from and OUT) or (wallet == to and IN). It never looks at the SMS kind or party. Every imported line starts UNCLASSIFIED and unconfirmed (RecordLines.kt:27-28), so a card PURCHASE qualifies. mergeManualIntoSms (lines 47-67) runs over all SMS lines of the batch. Scenario: owner enters by hand 'transfer 500 from bank A to account B' on 7 Oct; on 7 or 8 Oct a 500 riyal shop purchase SMS arrives on A before the bank's transfer message. The manual row is deleted, the purchase becomes a confirmed internal transfer (disappears from spending), an auto +50,000 leg lands on B; when the real transfer SMS arrives it is a plain unclassified OUT. Checked against golden shapes: 'حوالة داخلية صادرة / من:1111 / إلى:TEST PERSON / بـSR 500' (golden/sms.json line 15) does match when wallet A has last4 1111 and amount/day agree, so the happy path holds. It fails when: (1) another same-amount message arrives first (above); (2) the message is a cash withdrawal (todo-transfers-4); (3) the SMS amount includes the fee (golden line 169 shape with fees; legOfTransfer compares the raw row amount) -> no merge, transfer counted twice; (4) the manual date is more than 1 day off; (5) the party was earlier given the 'something else' label, which confirms incoming rows as other income before effects run (TransferParties.kt:265-270) -> legOfTransfer line 37 rejects -> counted twice and as income; (6) wallet A has no last 4 saved -> the message waits with the 'is this account yours?' question (SmsAccountLegs.kt:49) and nothing merges until answered. Test coverage: OwnTransferManualMergeTest builds body 'رسالة وهمية' with SmsKind.OTHER and calls OwnTransferLegEffect alone (lines 40-50); no real message text, no OwnAccountByLast4Effect/CashWithdrawalEffect/SmsFeeEffect in the chain, no competing purchase.
- impact: A real purchase can vanish from spending and a transfer can be counted twice; the release note 'passes tests' is true only for an invented message.
- fix: Failing tests first, through the full smsRecordEffects chain with golden message bodies: (a) manual 500 A->B, then a 500 purchase SMS on A: purchase must stay a purchase and the manual row must survive; (b) manual + 'حوالة داخلية صادرة' with a fee total line; (c) manual to cash + ATM message. Fix: restrict merge candidates to transfer-kind rows (sms.kind in LEG_KINDS, or CASH_WITHDRAWAL when the target is the cash wallet), compare against the principal after fee split, and when more than one candidate exists ask instead of taking firstOrNull.
- second reviewer: Real as coded: OwnTransferManualMerge.kt:35-41 checks only amount, currency, day, direction and wallet, imported lines start UNCLASSIFIED/unconfirmed (RecordLines.kt:27-28) and nothing in prepareRecordedLines (RecordLines.kt:83-100) confirms a purchase before the effect, and OwnTransferManualMergeTest.kt:40-50 uses body 'رسالة وهمية', SmsKind.OTHER and OwnTransferLegEffect alone. Lowered to medium because the purchase case needs an exact same-amount OUT on the same wallet within one day of a manual transfer (the frequent variant is the ATM one, finding 4); sub-cases 5 and 6 were not checked.

### arch-2 [Take the hide-numbers default out of core formatMoney and mask only at display] - Hide-numbers flag lives in core formatMoney, so saved assistant replies can be stored masked
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: confirmed | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/FormatMoney.kt:84`
- evidence: FormatMoney.kt:78-87: formatMoney has default parameter hidden = MoneyPrivacy.isHidden and returns the mask instead of the number. MoneyPrivacy (core/MoneyPrivacy.kt:8-13) is a global object whose reader is replaced by the UI (ui/theme/Appearance.kt:69). Use cases call formatMoney without passing hidden (14 call sites in app, 43 in core, none pass hidden=). ReplyBuilder.money in app/usecase/AssistantContext.kt:140 builds assistant reply text with it; the reply becomes AssistMessage.text (core/AssistModel.kt:168) and is persisted at app/usecase/AssistantChat.kt:136-141 (st.messages.save).
- impact: If the owner asks the assistant something while the eye (hide numbers) is on, the answer is saved to the account with dots instead of the amounts, and stays that way in history after the eye is turned off. The reverse also holds: replies saved while visible keep showing amounts when numbers are hidden. A display preference is changing stored data.
- fix: Remove the default from core: formatMoney(hidden = false) and apply masking only in the ui layer (Amount.kt and the t()/money helpers). For chat, keep storing real text and mask at render time (ReplyBuilder already collects amounts). Add a test that formats with the flag on and asserts the saved message contains the number.
- second reviewer: FormatMoney.kt:84 defaults hidden to MoneyPrivacy.isHidden, and ReplyBuilder.money at AssistantContext.kt:138-141 calls formatMoney(minor, c) without it; AssistantChat.kt:139-141 then saves the reply messages, and no app use case reads or overrides MoneyPrivacy. I did not open TurnKit to see the exact line where reply text becomes AssistMessage.text, so that last link rests on the reviewer's citation. Severity lowered to medium because the damage is limited to chat history text, not ledger data; AssistantStart.kt:79 has the same pattern.

### build-7 [Turn the Android lint check back on (with a baseline) before the store.] - Lint is switched off for release-type builds and there is no other static check in the build
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/androidApp/build.gradle.kts:98`
- evidence: Line 98: lint { checkReleaseBuilds = false } with a comment that release lint reads Kotlin with an older version and reports false errors. A search of all native-app *.kts and *.properties files finds no detekt, ktlint, kover, allWarningsAsErrors or other analysis configuration.
- impact: The checks Play cares about (missing or misdeclared permissions, exported components, new-API calls below minSdk 24, target SDK behaviour changes) are never run on the variant that ships. With SMS permissions and minSdk 24, an API-level crash on an old phone would be found by a tester instead of by the build.
- fix: After the toolchain upgrade (build-3) re-enable lint for the store variant; until then run :androidApp:lintDebug with a committed baseline file in CI so only new issues fail.

### sms-1 [Unify SMS timestamp source and block identical-text copies from the 60-second second-charge rule; add device tests] - Same SMS with a different timestamp is auto-recorded again as a 'second charge'
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/ImportPreviewStage.kt:121`
- evidence: The identity of a message includes its timestamp in both layers: the device queue id is sha(sender|timestamp|body) (device/.../SmsSafety.kt:92, SmsInboxStore.kt:177) and the ledger reference is 'SMS:'+hash(sender|receivedAt|body) (core/.../BankSmsModel.kt:243). The live receiver uses parts[0].timestampMillis (BankSmsReceiver.kt:44) while the inbox scan uses date_sent and falls back to date when date_sent is 0 (AndroidSmsInbox.kt:158). The scan always re-reads live messages because the receiver never advances the cursor. If the two timestamps differ (provider that stores date_sent=0, or messages restored onto a new phone, which SmsCatchUp.before() deliberately rewinds and re-reads: SmsCatchUp.kt:29-35), the copy is not DUPLICATE by reference; it becomes SIMILAR via smsSimilarity (core/.../Dedupe.kt:90-97) and then ImportPreviewStage.kt:120-123 flips SIMILAR to NEW when secondChargeWithinWindow is true. That function (core/.../DuplicateCharges.kt:69-75) compares the time written inside the message body, so an identical copy has a 0-second difference, passes the <=60s test and is recorded automatically (only a 'duplicate?' flag is raised). No test references date_sent or timestampMillis (only the two main files contain them).
- impact: On an affected phone, or after moving to a new phone, purchases that carry a time and merchant can be recorded twice without the owner pressing anything, so spending is overstated and the wallet balance is understated until he answers each 'duplicate?' question.
- fix: Make message identity independent of which path read it: use one timestamp source (date_sent, with the same fallback, in both receiver and scan) and add a second duplicate check on sender + normalised body when the body itself carries a date and time. In secondChargeWithinWindow, do not treat a row whose redacted description is identical to the older transaction's rawDescription as a second charge (keep it SIMILAR or DUPLICATE). Add an on-device test that receives a message live and then scans it, and one that re-reads restored messages.
- second reviewer: The code path is real: ImportPreviewStage.kt:120-123 flips an SMS SIMILAR to NEW, and secondChargeWithinWindow (DuplicateCharges.kt:69-75) returns true for an identical copy (0-second gap); the reference includes receivedAt (BankSmsModel.kt:243) and no body-only check exists. The trigger is unproven: it needs the provider's date_sent to be 0 or differ from the live timestampMillis (BankSmsReceiver.kt:44 vs AndroidSmsInbox.kt:158), which on standard Android are the same value, and it only hits purchases with a parsed time and merchant. The proposed fix works because rawDescription equals row.description (RecordLines.kt:40), but unifying the timestamp source alone does not cover date_sent=0 after a restore, so the body-based check is the essential part.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: When two bank messages have exactly the same text and time, should the app treat the second as a copy (drop it) or as a real second charge (record and ask)?

### countries-6 [Use country day for importedAt in recordedToday] - 'Recorded today' list mixes UTC import dates with the country day
- severity: low | verdict: confirmed | urgency: later | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/wiring/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/wiring/SmsGraph.kt:240`
- evidence: recordedToday() takes today = c.env.today() (country day, line 228) and compares it with b.importedAt.take(10) (line 231 against yesterday, line 240 against today). importedAt is treated here as a text timestamp and cut to its first 10 characters, which is the UTC date.
- impact: On 2026-11-15 at 01:00 Riyadh (UTC 2026-11-14 22:00) the owner confirms a waiting message whose operation date is 13 Nov. occurredAt is not today, and importedAt.take(10) is '2026-11-14', not '2026-11-15', so the operation does not appear under 'recorded today' although he just recorded it. After 03:00 the same action shows correctly. Display only; no money is affected.
- fix: Convert importedAt with dayIn(countryTimeZone, importedAt) before comparing.
- second reviewer: SmsGraph.kt:228 takes the country day while lines 231 and 240 compare it with importedAt.take(10); importedAt is clock.nowIso() (ImportStatement.kt:92), which Env.kt:112-115 formats as UTC with 'Z'. Between 00:00 and 03:00 Riyadh an operation confirmed now with an older occurredAt is missing from 'recorded today'; display only, and dayIn(timeZone, importedAt) fixes it.

### sec-firebase-9 [Verify deployed rules match firestore.rules and fix stale comments] - Rules file comments say 'draft, not deployed' while the handover says deployed; nothing proves the live rules equal the repo
- severity: low | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `firestore.rules:33`
- evidence: firestore.rules:33 (RULES-4) and :54 (inbox) both carry 'ما اتنشرتش، النشر بموافقة المالك'. HANDOVER.md:154 says the rules were published on the project for 0.5.5 (§118-ب-3). The two statements contradict, and the next AI session starts from files only.
- impact: If the inbox block is not actually live, every 'send to us' message is rejected; if the digit rule is not live, shared merchant names with long numbers are accepted. A later session may also redeploy or skip deployment based on the stale comment.
- fix: Compare the console rules with the file once, then update the two comments with the deploy date; record the deployed commit hash in HANDOVER.
- second reviewer: firestore.rules:33 and :54 both still say 'draft, not deployed, deploy with owner approval', while HANDOVER.md:154 says the rules were published on masroufy-d4b96 for 0.5.5 (§118-ب-3). The contradiction is real; which one is true can only be settled in the console, which I cannot see.

### release-3 [Verify what SMS text is uploaded and correct the permission sheet wording] - SMS permission sheet says messages are read 'on your phone' while code comments say redacted bank text is stored and restored
- severity: high | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/TextsAreaImportsSettings.kt:44`
- evidence: Line 44: SMS_PERM_WHY = 'We read only the bank messages you choose, on your phone.' Lines 45-46 say the permission is asked once and understood messages are recorded automatically. Nothing on the sheet (BankSmsSettingsParts.kt:85-98) says anything is uploaded. But SmsTransferParties.kt:6 states 'the stored description = the message text after cutting the digits (last 4 only)', and CODEMAP line 761 (RestoreForm.kt, PRIVACY-3) describes 'protected collections (containing bank text)'. I did not open the Firestore mapper to confirm the field reaches the server.
- impact: Play's user data policy requires a prominent in-app disclosure that says what is collected and where it goes, shown before the system permission dialog. A sheet that implies data stays on the phone while message text is synced to the account is the classic reason SMS declarations are rejected, and it is misleading to users.
- fix: Confirm exactly which SMS-derived fields are written to Firestore. Then reword the sheet: which messages are read, that the redacted text and extracted amounts are saved to the user's account on Firebase, that nothing else is read or shared, with a link to the privacy policy and an explicit agree button.

### sync-4 [Wait for pending writes (or warn) before clearing the local copy on sign-out.] - Sign-out wipes the local Firestore cache without waiting for queued offline writes
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/FirebaseSetup.kt:31`
- evidence: wipeLocalCopy() calls old.terminate() then old.clearPersistence() (lines 31-38); AndroidSignOutCleanup.afterSignOut calls it unconditionally (AndroidSignOutCleanup.kt:18-20). Writes are queued locally and not awaited while the session runs (FirestoreSpace.kt:38-42 and 53-65, 'outbox'). A grep for waitForPendingWrites over native-app main sources returned nothing, and SignIn.kt (lines 61-70, 111-113) only calls beforeSignOut/afterSignOut with no pending-write or online check.
- impact: A user who recorded expenses with no or weak network and then signs out (or deletes and re-signs to fix a problem) loses every write that had not reached the server yet, with no warning. The money entries simply never existed on the server.
- fix: Before terminate, call waitForPendingWrites with a timeout; if it does not finish (offline), stop and tell the user that N changes are not uploaded yet and ask to confirm or retry when online.
- second reviewer: SignIn.kt:110-114 runs beforeSignOut, auth.signOut(), afterSignOut with no pending-write or online check, and FirebaseSetup.kt:31-38 does terminate() then clearPersistence(), which drops the SDK's queued writes; grep for waitForPendingWrites in main sources returns nothing. Worse than stated: the sign-out sheet (AccountSheets.kt:85, text at TextsMoreAccount.kt:76) tells the user the data is saved in the account and returns in full, which is false for writes still queued offline.

### tests-5 [Warn when RealStatementTest is excluded; add committed synthetic statement fixture] - Acceptance totals and balance chain are proven in Kotlin, but the test vanishes silently when the owner file is missing
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built
- where: `native-app/core/build.gradle.kts:48`
- evidence: RealStatementTest.kt:21-44 asserts 1912 rows, 302,171.45 / 201,703.18 / 100,468.27 / 300,040.87, closing 2,707.25 @ 2026-09-04 and zero per-line mismatches: exactly the CLAUDE.md criteria. core/build.gradle.kts:48 `else filter { excludeTestsMatching("app.masroufy.core.RealStatementTest") }` drops it with no log line when files/transactions_full.csv is not found (the two PDF tests at lines 52 and 57 do print a warning; this one does not). The same pattern is in data/build.gradle.kts:46-50. CI never has the file (workflow comment line 3).
- impact: On CI, on another machine, or in a worktree where the lookup of the owner's files folder fails, the build reports success without the single most important money check having run, and nothing tells the reader.
- fix: Log a loud warning in the else branch and print in the test report whether RealStatementTest ran. Add a synthetic, committed statement fixture (invented data, a few hundred rows with a stated-balance column) with its own fixed totals so the same code path is exercised in CI. In the release checklist require the line 'RealStatementTest: 1 test, 0 failures' from the XML.

### sync-5 [Wire FirestoreSpace.writeFailures to a user-visible message.] - Server-rejected writes are never shown: writeFailures has no collector and the mirror already shows the change
- severity: medium | verdict: confirmed | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/firestore/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/firestore/FirestoreSpace.kt:47`
- evidence: write() launches the commit and on failure only does failures.tryEmit(e) and Problems.report (lines 53-65); the comment on line 46 says the screen must show it. A grep for 'writeFailures' across all Kotlin files returns only its declaration (FirestoreSpace.kt:47) and a comment (FirestoreSync.kt:68). Meanwhile saveAll/deleteAll/updateDoc apply the change to LocalMirror immediately regardless of the outcome (lines 107, 116, 131).
- impact: When a write is refused (security rules, update() on a document deleted from another phone, a document over the size limit, quota exhausted for writes), the user saw success, the screen shows the new value, and later the value silently reverts when the listener catches up. Follow-up steps of the same use case have already run on the assumption that the first write held.
- fix: Collect writeFailures of the account space and of each country space in the app container and surface a visible, non-dismissable-until-read message ('a change was not saved'), then trigger a re-read (changes.changed()).
- second reviewer: FirestoreSpace.kt:47 writeFailures has no collector anywhere in main sources (grep finds only the declaration and a comment at FirestoreSync.kt:68), while saveAll/deleteAll/updateDoc update the mirror right after launching the write (FirestoreSpace.kt:106-107, 115-116, 130-131). The only trace is Problems.report(SYNC_WRITE_FAILED) at FirestoreSpace.kt:62, which goes to the diagnostic problem log; no SYNC_WRITE_FAILED handling exists in ui/commonMain, so the user is not told on the screen where the change was made.

### arch-8 [Wire or hide appLanguage and cashQuestion; add an unwired-hook test] - Optional screen dependencies default to null; two are drawn but never wired
- severity: low | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built

- evidence: ui Deps interfaces contain 47 properties of the form 'val x: T? get() = null'. A scripted check for 'override val <name>' in wiring found two with no override anywhere in wiring or androidApp: appLanguage (MoreHooks.kt:32, read in AppSettingsScreen.kt:137) and cashQuestion (MoreHooks.kt:44). NotificationSettingsScreen.kt:98-99 still renders the cash-question row and on tap runs 'more.cashQuestion?.choose(every)' then sets cash = every locally.
- impact: The compiler cannot tell a finished feature from a drawn one: forgetting to wire a hook produces no error, only a control that silently does nothing (project rule 15). The cash-question setting may look changed on screen without being saved.
- fix: Wire or hide these two; for the pattern, add one test in wiring/jvmTest that walks every nullable hook of SpaceDeps and fails on unexpected nulls, with an explicit allow-list.
- second reviewer: appLanguage (MoreHooks.kt:32) and cashQuestion (MoreHooks.kt:44) have no override anywhere in the Kotlin sources, so they are unwired. But neither is a silent dead control: NotificationSettingsScreen.kt:98 passes more.cashQuestion != null as editable and line 131 disables the switch, and AppSettingsScreen.kt:146-147 locks the English row with a 'soon' label by a recorded fix (r06-fix055b). The pattern risk and the value of an unwired-hook test remain.

### tests-8 [Wire secretscan.mjs into CI and a pre-push hook] - Secret scan is a manual script; nothing in CI or a git hook enforces it on a public repo
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built
- where: `HANDOVER.md:155`
- evidence: scripts/secretscan.mjs exists. grep for 'secretscan' in package.json, .github/ and scripts/build-android.ps1 finds nothing; the only references are HANDOVER prose (line 155: 'before it, secretscan on the range was clean'). .github/workflows contains only native-core.yml and prices.yml. `git config core.hooksPath` is empty and there is no .husky/.githooks folder. In-code guards do exist for logs (app/src/jvmTest/.../perf/NoSecretsInLogsTest.kt).
- impact: Pushes are made by rotating AI sessions; one session forgetting the manual step can publish a key, a real statement line or a full account number to a public repository, which cannot be undone.
- fix: Add a CI job (ubuntu, no secrets) that runs `node scripts/secretscan.mjs` on the pushed range and fails the run, and a committed pre-push hook (core.hooksPath) that runs the same script locally.

### errors-1 [Wire writeFailures/errors to a visible banner and keep the SMS when its write fails] - Server-rejected writes are swallowed: screen says saved, nobody listens to writeFailures
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: confirmed | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/firestore/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/firestore/FirestoreSpace.kt:53`
- evidence: FirestoreSpace.write() (lines 53-65) launches the commit in writeScope and returns immediately; on failure it only does failures.tryEmit(e) + Problems.report(SYNC_WRITE_FAILED). The doc comment on writeFailures (line 46) says the screen must show it, but a grep over all production source for `writeFailures` finds only its definition (line 47) and a comment in FirestoreSync.kt:68 - zero collectors. FirestoreSync.errors (FirestoreSync.kt:50) likewise has no collector. saveAll (lines 101-108) applies the write to the in-memory mirror right after launching, so every later read and the success toast see the data. In ReviewSmsInbox.kt:204-207 the bank SMS is acknowledged (removed from the inbox) right after importer.commit returns, i.e. before any server confirmation.
- impact: If the server permanently rejects a write (security rules after a rules change, invalid or oversized document, account state), the user was already told it was saved; the listener later rolls the document back and the operation silently disappears, with the source SMS already removed from the inbox. The only trace is a line in the problem log. Balance then differs from what the user saw, with no explanation.
- fix: Collect writeFailures of the active space and the account space in the app shell (through AppDeps, next to remoteChanges) and show a persistent banner/toast such as 'a change was not saved' and trigger a reload. For SMS auto-record, keep the message in the inbox (or re-queue it) when the batch write fails.
- second reviewer: FirestoreSpace.kt:53-65 launches the commit and only does failures.tryEmit + Problems.report; a grep of all native-app Kotlin finds writeFailures only at its definition (line 47) and a comment (FirestoreSync.kt:68), and FirestoreSync.errors (line 50) has no collector either. ReviewSmsInbox.kt:193-207 acknowledges the message right after importer.commit returns (auto-record defers via ackLater but still does not wait for the server). Mechanism and fix are right; severity lowered to medium because it needs a permanent server rejection, which is rare for a single-owner account with stable rules, and no owner decision in the .md files covers it.

### release-1 [Write and host the privacy policy and link it in sign-in, Settings and the SMS sheet] - No privacy policy exists, in the app or as a hosted page
- severity: high | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: not_built
- where: `PLAN-0.6.md:543`
- evidence: PLAN-0.6.md:543 lists 'a line in the privacy policy and the Play data safety page' as still required. A grep of all Kotlin under native-app for privacy policy / terms wording (Arabic and English) returned only unrelated debt-term keys. A grep of ui commonMain for any http link or policy text returned nothing relevant. git ls-files has no privacy page (the only 'privacy' hits are MoneyPrivacy.kt and a test). Meanwhile the app requests READ_SMS and RECEIVE_SMS (native-app/device/src/androidMain/AndroidManifest.xml:4-5), uses Firebase Auth and Firestore, Analytics and Crashlytics.
- impact: Google Play requires a privacy policy link in the listing and inside the app for any app using sensitive permissions; the SMS permissions declaration cannot pass without one. Testers in Saudi Arabia and Egypt are also handing over bank data with no written notice of what is collected.
- fix: Write one policy page in Arabic and English covering: bank SMS content, transactions, names of other people and their debts, email and Google account, usage events, crash reports with account id, Firebase as processor and where data is stored, retention, deletion path, and a contact. Host it on a stable URL. Link it on the sign-in screen, in Settings, and on the SMS permission sheet. Use the same inventory to fill the Play Data safety form.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Who is the named data controller (owner as an individual or a company) and what contact address goes in the policy?

### build-6 [Write one build script and a clean-machine checklist for the new app.] - scripts/build-android.ps1 builds the frozen old app; the Kotlin app has no build script and its toolchain lives outside the repo
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: now | effort: S | status: not_built
- where: `scripts/build-android.ps1:29`
- evidence: Line 25 runs 'npm.cmd run android:sync', line 29 runs ./gradlew.bat :app:assembleDebug inside android/, line 52 checks package app.masroufy.personal: this is the old Capacitor app, not native-app (applicationId app.masroufy.mobile). Lines 7-11 take JAVA_HOME, ANDROID_HOME, GRADLE_USER_HOME and the trial keystore from ~/Documents/Codex/android-build-tools. For the Kotlin app the only instructions are prose in HANDOVER.md lines 29-31 with a hardcoded user-profile JDK path (jdk-21.0.12.1+1). No build file declares a JVM toolchain, and no local.properties exists in native-app (it is gitignored), so the Android target of ui/wiring/device/firestore is enabled only when ANDROID_HOME is set (e.g. ui/build.gradle.kts lines 16-21).
- impact: The next model on a clean machine has to reconstruct by hand: JDK 21, Android SDK with platform 36 and build-tools, ANDROID_HOME, secrets/google-services.json, the debug keystore, the owner's files/ folder, and optionally MASROUFY_BUILD_ROOT. The script whose name promises an Android build produces the wrong, frozen app. Old-app safeguards (certificate comparison, versionCode must increase, SHA-256 printout) do not exist for the new app.
- fix: Add scripts/build-native.ps1 for native-app that checks prerequisites up front (JDK 21, ANDROID_HOME, secrets file, registered signing fingerprint), runs assembleFast, then applies the same checks the old script has (certificate equals previous APK, versionCode increased, prints SHA-256). Rename or header-mark build-android.ps1 as old-app only. Add a short 'clean machine checklist' to HANDOVER.

### tests-9 [Write RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md and commit smoke scripts] - Release smoke is not a repeatable checklist; the scripts it cites are not in the repo
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built
- where: `HANDOVER.md:341`
- evidence: HANDOVER.md:198, :341 and :387 cite `smoke/speed.sh` and `smoke/runs/speed.txt` as the speed evidence. No `smoke` directory exists in the worktree or under native-app, `git ls-files` has no smoke/ path. No file named *smoke* or *checklist* is tracked other than two unit tests. Each release's smoke items (a)..(j) are written fresh in the HANDOVER entry of that round; HANDOVER.md:217 lists 'running the app itself on the emulator after the fixes' as not tried for the last fix round.
- impact: The next model or person cannot rerun the same smoke: items change per round and the timing script is lost with the session that wrote it. A release can ship with a different, thinner check than the previous one without anyone noticing.
- fix: Commit one RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md (fixed numbered items: erase account leaves zero docs, add/delete/undo per entity, import + revert, SMS read on large inbox with time limit, both countries, backup/restore, full instrumented run, RealStatementTest ran, CI green, secretscan) and commit the smoke scripts under scripts/smoke. Each release entry in HANDOVER then only records pass/fail per number.

### money-4 [إثبات تشغيل اختبار القبول قبل كل إصدار] - Acceptance totals are proven only on the owner's machine; the test is silently excluded elsewhere
- severity: low (finder said medium) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/core/build.gradle.kts:48`
- evidence: core/src/jvmTest/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/RealStatementTest.kt:28-31 asserts 302,171.45 / 201,703.18 / 100,468.27 / 300,040.87, and :40-43 asserts the chain from 4837.83 @ 2025-01-01 to 2,707.25 @ 2026-09-04 with 0 mismatches. But build.gradle.kts:44-48 only sets the file path when files/transactions_full.csv exists, otherwise `filter { excludeTestsMatching("app.masroufy.core.RealStatementTest") }` - the run stays green without it. Grep for 30217145 / 270725 / 483783 as integer literals across native-app returned nothing, so there is no committed fixture-based copy of these criteria.
- impact: GitHub Actions and any AI model working in a worktree without the private file get a green build that never checked the three acceptance criteria; a regression in classification or parsing can ship unnoticed until the owner runs tests locally.
- fix: Keep the private file private, but make the skip loud (fail a dedicated 'acceptance' task or print a summary line that the release checklist requires), and record in HANDOVER the last commit where the test actually ran. Optionally commit a hashed/anonymised amounts-only fixture that reproduces the four totals and the closing balance.
- second reviewer: core/build.gradle.kts:48 does exclude RealStatementTest when the file is missing and the build stays green, but it is not silent: native-app/build.gradle.kts:38-43 searches upward from any worktree for files/transactions_full.csv and prints a lifecycle warning when absent. Real remaining gap is CI and machines without the private file, with no committed fixture copy of the totals; the finding itself marks this by_design.

### money-3 [تحذير عند خانة رصيد مش مقروءة] - Unreadable balance cell in a statement is silently treated as 'no balance'
- severity: low (finder said medium) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/ImportSchemas.kt:113`
- evidence: Line 113: `val balance = if (balanceRaw == null || trim(balanceRaw).isEmpty()) null else tryParseMoney(balanceRaw)`. tryParseMoney returns null on any format error, and null means 'no stated balance' - reconcileBalance (core/Reconcile.kt:58) then skips that row (`?: return@forEachIndexed`) and it is not counted in checkedCount nor reported as an error. Debit/credit cells in the same function do fail loudly (lines 106-107).
- impact: A statement whose balance column is in a format the parser rejects (three decimals, dot thousands, a currency suffix) imports with 'zero mismatches' because nothing was compared; the owner believes the balance chain was verified when it was not.
- fix: Distinguish empty from malformed: for a non-empty cell that fails to parse, add a RowError (or a per-file warning count 'N balance cells unreadable') and show checkedCount versus row count on the import result.
- second reviewer: ImportSchemas.kt:113 does turn a malformed balance cell into null and Reconcile.kt:58 skips it without an error. But the result is not presented as fully verified: ReconcileState.kt:24 gives no verdict when checkedCount is 0, and :48-49 print movement count, checked count and the count of rows without a stated balance. Remaining gap is only that 'unreadable' is not distinguished from 'empty'.

### money-2 [توحيد قاعدة العملة في سلسلة الرصيد] - Wallet balance adds transactions whose currency differs from the wallet
- severity: low (finder said medium) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/ZakatHawl.kt:21`
- evidence: walletEvents (lines 19-25) adds t.amountMinor to the wallet for every transaction on that wallet with no comparison of t.currency to wallet.currency; walletBalancesOn (line 35) is the source of the hero number. BalanceWhy.kt:150-152 does the same: `add(source, direction, t.amountMinor)` first and only then `if (t.currency != wallet.currency) other += item` - i.e. the foreign-currency row is flagged for review but still counted. LoadWithYouNow.kt:38-46 lists all wallets and all rows with no currency filter, while LoadHomeCardData.kt:77-79 (daily allowance) filters both wallets and rows by currency - so the two home numbers can disagree for the same data.
- impact: If a row in another currency lands on a wallet (the code has a dedicated review list for it, so it is expected to happen), a pound amount is added as if it were riyals: the hero balance, zakat series and savings-goal balance are wrong, and the daily-allowance card uses a different total than the hero.
- fix: Pick one rule and apply it in walletEvents: skip (and surface) rows where t.currency != wallet.currency, or block them at write time in every write path (EditTransaction already blocks at line 142; import/SMS paths not verified). Make LoadWithYouNow and LoadDailyAllowance share the same filtered input.
- second reviewer: ZakatHawl.kt:19-25 does add foreign-currency rows unfiltered, and BalanceWhy.kt:49 documents this as known ("walletBalancesOn ما بيفرّقش") and surfaces it in the review list. Reachability is narrow: ManageWallets.kt:112 creates every wallet in the space currency, SMS writes use wallet.currency (ForeignSmsAsks.kt:141, CoveredSmsRepair.kt:149), EditTransaction.kt:142 blocks. The one open write path I found is AddTransaction.kt:110 (`input.currency ?: wallet.currency`, no check), fed by assistant cards (AssistantActions.kt:182, AssistantMoneyCards.kt:131, AssistantSplit.kt:101); the fix should add the check there. Hero versus allowance mismatch (LoadWithYouNow.kt:41 vs LoadHomeCardData.kt:79) only occurs for such a row.

### money-6 [حماية rateOfMoney من الطفح] - rateOfMoney multiplies two Longs with no overflow check
- severity: low | verdict: confirmed | urgency: later | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/Money.kt:140`
- evidence: Line 140: `val num = abs(a) * abs(numerator)` - plain Long multiply; only the final result is range-checked (line 146). Most callers pass small numerators (1000, days), but core/Installments.kt:103 calls `rateOfMoney(financingCostOf(p), paidMinor, p.totalMinor)` - money times money. The safe alternative already exists (128-bit divideRounded in Quantity.kt:118, mulDivWide in GrowthMath.kt:38).
- impact: Wraps silently once cost x paid exceeds about 9.2e18 minor units squared (roughly 30 million x 30 million). Not reachable for normal personal loans, but a mistyped total or a very large Egyptian-pound financing would give a garbage or negative 'financing cost' instead of an error.
- fix: Route rateOfMoney through the 128-bit multiply/divide used by shareOfAmount, or guard with the same check as multiplyMoneyByInt (Money.kt:129).
- second reviewer: Money.kt:140 multiplies abs(a) * abs(numerator) as plain Long with only `a` range-checked (line 137) and the result checked after the fact (line 146); line 144 `rem * 2` can also wrap. Installments.kt:103 passes money x money, guarded only by paid <= total (line 100), so overflow needs roughly 30 million x 30 million in major units: unrealistic but unguarded.

### money-5 [شيل Double من متوسط ووسيط الميزانية] - Floating point used in money averages and medians (rule 1 violation)
- severity: low | verdict: confirmed | urgency: later | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/Budget.kt:75`
- evidence: Line 75: `JsText.round(total.toDouble() / used.size).toLong()` computes the average completed-period spend (feeds 'use the average' for budget limits via lastPeriodsAverage at :85-88). Line 107: `JsText.round((sorted[mid - 1] + sorted[mid]).toDouble() / 2).toLong()` computes the median used by anomaly detection. Grep also shows backup validation holding money sums in `HashMap<Any?, Double>` (core/BackupCheck.kt:222-224). All other Double hits in core/app are colours, PDF coordinates, percent display (FormatMoney.kt:100-106, documented) or JSON number typing.
- impact: Results are exact for realistic sizes (below 2^53), so no wrong number today; but it breaks the project's own hard rule and negative half-values round differently from rateOfMoney (JS round goes toward +infinity, rateOfMoney away from zero).
- fix: Replace with rateOfMoney(total, 1, used.size) and rateOfMoney(a + b, 1, 2); in BackupCheck sum as Long after isSafeInteger.
- second reviewer: Budget.kt:75 (`total.toDouble() / used.size`) and Budget.kt:107 (median via toDouble()/2) use floating point on money, and BackupCheck.kt:222-224 sums amounts in HashMap<Any?, Double>. Results are exact below 2^53, so it is a rule-1 violation with no wrong number today, as the finding states.

### money-1 [قفل قراية الفاصلة في parseMoney + حالات اختبار] - Comma typed as decimal mark multiplies the amount by 100 (manual entry and CSV import)
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/Money.kt:56`
- evidence: parseMoney line 56 does `s.filterNot { isWhitespace(it) || it == ',' }` with no check that commas sit every 3 digits, so "12,50" becomes digits "1250" => 1,250.00 and "64,25" => 6,425.00. Amount fields only run normalizeDigits on typed text (native-app/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/ui/components/Fields.kt:60) and then call tryParseMoney directly (e.g. ui/screens/more/BalanceUpdateSheet.kt:65, dues/AddDebtSheet.kt:144, budgets/LimitForm.kt:59). CSV import uses the same parser (core/ImportSchemas.kt:78 and :104-105). The SMS reader documents this exact bug and rejects it (core/SmsAmountTokens.kt:7 "SAR 64,25 was recorded as 6,425" and the GROUPING regex at :44), and the FX rate parser in the same debt screen treats comma as a decimal (core/FxSettle.kt:32) - so the same keystroke means two different things.
- impact: A user whose keypad offers a comma key (common on Android decimal keypads, and normal habit for many Egyptian/European users) saves a balance, debt, budget or transaction 100 times too large with no error. A statement exported with comma decimals imports with every amount x100.
- fix: In parseMoney validate grouping before stripping: accept commas only in the shape \d{1,3}(,\d{3})+ (optionally followed by .dd); treat a single comma followed by 1-2 digits and no dot as a decimal mark or reject it with MONEY_BAD_FORMAT. Add golden cases ("12,50", "1,234.50", "1,23", "12,5"). Decide with the owner whether "12,50" means 12.50 or is an error.
- second reviewer: native-app/core/.../Money.kt:56 strips every comma before validation, so "12,50" parses as 1,250.00; Fields.kt:60 only runs normalizeDigits and ImportSchemas.kt:78/104-105 use the same parser. No guard found, and OVERRIDES.md:1613 shows the owner already treats "64,25" as invalid for SMS. Extra for the fix: native-app/golden/money.json:28 and :88 lock the wrong behaviour ("1 234,00" => 12340000), so the golden reference must change with the parser.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: «12,50» تتقري 12.50 ولا تترفض برسالة خطأ؟
