### todo-transfers-4 - Manual bank-to-cash transfer plus the ATM message credits the cash wallet twice
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: S
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/CashWithdrawalEffect.kt:34`
- evidence: Effect order in SmsLaneEffects.kt:34-38 is OwnAccountByLast4Effect, OwnTransferLegEffect, SmsSalaryEffect, CashWithdrawalEffect. Inside OwnTransferLegEffect, mergeManualIntoSms (OwnTransferManualMerge.kt:53-66) sees the ATM line (OUT on bank wallet, same amount, transferToWalletId still null), deletes the manual row and adds an automatic IN leg on the manual row's target = the cash wallet. Then CashWithdrawalEffect lines 27-37 selects the same line (sms.movesToCash and OUT, no check that it was already merged) and sets transferToWalletId = cash.id. The balance adds a transferToWalletId row as incoming on that wallet (core/ZakatHawl.kt:24) and also adds the auto leg (line 21). With 1,000 withdrawn: bank -100,000, cash +100,000 (leg) +100,000 (transferTo) = cash shows 1,000 extra. No test combines the two effects (OwnTransferManualMergeTest runs OwnTransferLegEffect only).
- impact: Cash wallet and total are overstated by the withdrawal amount every time the owner logs a withdrawal by hand before the bank message is recorded.
- fix: Failing test first: manual transfer bank->cash 100,000 via AddTransaction, then record a CASH_WITHDRAWAL SMS through the full smsRecordEffects list; assert cash rose by exactly 100,000 and total is unchanged. Fix: in mergeManualIntoSms, when the line is a cash withdrawal that will move to the same cash wallet, delete the manual row and add NO auto leg (let CashWithdrawalEffect set transferToWalletId); or make CashWithdrawalEffect skip lines that already have a '#own-leg' in ctx.extra.
- second reviewer: SmsLaneEffects.kt:36-38 runs OwnTransferLegEffect before CashWithdrawalEffect; mergeManualIntoSms (OwnTransferManualMerge.kt:53-66) matches the ATM line while its transferToWalletId is still null, deletes the manual row and adds an IN '#own-leg' on the cash wallet, then CashWithdrawalEffect.kt:27-37 sets transferToWalletId=cash with no check for an existing leg. core/ZakatHawl.kt:21 and :24 then credit cash twice; no test wires both effects with a manual transfer (SmsCashWithdrawalTest/SmsFeeEffectTest lists omit OwnTransferLegEffect).

### todo-transfers-5 - Real 'to: NAME' transfer messages with no digits are never treated as own account, and 'this is my other account' adds no second leg
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: before_release | effort: M
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/ManageTransfers.kt:184`
- evidence: otherLegs() starts with 'val last4 = party.last4 ?: return emptyList()' (line 184), so a party known only by name gets its rows marked INTERNAL_TRANSFER by decide() (lines 161-172) but no leg on the other wallet. At record time OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt:39-45 builds candidates only from digits, and OwnAccountAskSource.kt:83 requires party.last4, so a name-only party is neither auto-confirmed nor asked about. The golden Al Rajhi shapes are exactly name-only: 'حوالة داخلية صادرة / من:1111 / إلى:TEST PERSON / بـSR 500' (golden/sms.json line 15) and 'حوالة محلية واردة / من:TEST PERSON / ... / إلى:1111' (line 17). Scenario: owner sends 10,000 from A to his account in another bank, message shows his own name as beneficiary. Row stays unclassified OUT on A; he opens the transfers zone and answers 'my other account' -> row becomes internal transfer, wallet B gets nothing -> total is short by 1,000,000 unless bank B's own message is also recorded. OwnTransferLegEffectTest only uses digit-only bodies ('الى:2222', line 28).
- impact: For the most common real message shape the automatic second leg (owner decision §112-3) never happens, so the total drops by the transfer amount.
- fix: Failing test first: decide OWN_ACCOUNT for a name-only party with one OUT row of 1,000,000 on A and assert a leg appears on the chosen wallet. Fix: when the party has no digits, the 'my other account' answer must ask which wallet it is and store that wallet id on the TransferParty decision; otherLegs and addLegs then use the stored wallet instead of last4.
- second reviewer: ManageTransfers.kt:184 returns no legs when party.last4 is null and markOwnAccount (line 112, called from ui TransferPartyCard.kt:142 and TransfersScreen.kt:104) takes no wallet choice, so decide() only flips the kind; golden/sms.json:15 'إلى:TEST PERSON' yields a name-only party because ACCOUNT_OUT in core/SmsTransferParties.kt:55-56 needs a digit or mask after 'إلى'. OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt:39-45 and OwnAccountAskSource line 83 both need digits, so the OUT stays one-sided unless bank B's own message is recorded.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: لما تقول على اسم في التحويلات «ده حسابي التاني» والرسالة مفيهاش أرقام: نسألك وقتها «أنهي حساب من حساباتك؟» ونحفظ الإجابة؟

### todo-types-3 - Salary from a known employer account is not recognised at record time: payday is not passed
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/RecordLines.kt:94`
- evidence: Line 94 calls applyKnownPayerSalary(transactions[i], payers, now) with no fourth argument, so profilePayday is null (signature IncomeSignals.kt:71). Inside, salaryByPayerAllowed (PayerKinds.kt:52-57) ends with nearPayday(t.occurredAt, employer.expectedDayOfMonth ?: profilePayday), and nearPayday returns false for a null day (PayerKinds.kt:37). A monthly job normally has no day of its own - the model says its day is the profile payday (IncomeSources.kt:109) and the employer form never sets one (ManageEmployers.kt:69-71). The screen-side check does pass the payday (PayerKindAnswers.kt:98-99, IncomeSourceSignals.kt:75-79). Scenario: employer with expected salary 12,000.00, no own day, profile payday 28; SMS deposit of 12,000.00 on 2026-10-27 from the linked account, bank text without the word salary. Record time: amount matches, date check gets null -> not salary -> stays UNCLASSIFIED and is counted as a pending question (IncomingAskSource.kt:26-28). On screen the same transaction evaluates as salary-like (asks = false) but nothing writes it. The helper that would fix it later, IncomeSourceSignals.applyKnownPayers (line 94), has no caller in commonMain/androidMain (grep returned only its definition). Test: PayerKindsFlowTest covers the core rule with payday = 28 injected into PayerKindAnswers (line 43) and a 'noDay' case (line 84), but I found no test that goes through prepareRecordedLines with a profile payday.
- impact: Every month the real salary lands as an unanswered question and is excluded from income until the owner confirms it by hand. This is the most direct reproduction of 'it asks me again every time' for the salary.
- fix: Failing test first: memory container, profile payday 28, employer with payerKeys=[K], expectedMinor=1_200_000, expectedDayOfMonth=null; import one SMS/statement line of 12,000.00 dated the 27th from K through the real record path; assert economicKind == SALARY and economicKindConfirmed. Fix: give ImportStatementDeps a payday provider (profile repository) and pass it at RecordLines.kt:94; do the same wherever the background cycle builds these deps (BackgroundWiring.kt:30).
- second reviewer: RecordLines.kt:94 calls applyKnownPayerSalary without profilePayday, ImportStatementDeps (ImportTypes.kt:81-107) has no profile/payday field, and importDeps (BackgroundWiring.kt:28-31, also used by ImportsGraph.kt:52) passes none, so PayerKinds.kt:56 gets null and nearPayday returns false at line 37. OVERRIDES.md:2430 documents this as a known limitation ('the profile is not connected to the record path') and says the profile day is used in 'ask/apply', but IncomeSourceSignals.applyKnownPayers (line 94) has no production caller (tests only) and answerPayer (line 79) runs once at link time, so nothing later repairs the row; the only passing import test sets expectedDayOfMonth = 27 by hand (IncomeSignalsFlowTest.kt:117). Scope limit: it only bites when the employer has an expectedMinor and no own day and the text lacks a salary word; the row then stays pending per EstimatedKinds.kt:80-85.

### todo-assets-2 - A sale dated before the day the asset was added to the app is rejected as 'selling more than held (0)'
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/AssetSaleGain.kt:16`
- evidence: AddHolding.kt:80 always creates the opening lot with purchasedAt = today (the day of typing; there is no purchase-date field). saleOutcome (AssetSaleGain.kt:14-22) only counts lots with purchasedAt <= sale.soldAt and throws ASSET_SELL_OVER_HELD when quantity > held; computePosition (Assets.kt:125-142) replays events by date and throws the same. The sell sheet offers only Today/Yesterday chips (AssetTradeSheet.kt:194-195) and, when a bank credit is linked, FORCES the sale date to the credit's date (AssetTradeSheet.kt:153: day = op.date); candidates are credits within +-7 days (SellAsset.kt:63,92). Scenario: on 2026-11-14 the owner adds 'gold 10 g, bought for 3,000.00' (lot dated 2026-11-14). He sold it and the bank credit of 3,200.00 arrived 2026-11-12. He opens Sell, picks the bank, picks the credit -> day becomes 2026-11-12 -> held before that date = 0 -> red message 'selling 10, held 0' and save refuses. Same if he just taps 'Yesterday' on an item added today. Not covered: SellAssetTest.kt:52-53 always buys on 2026-01-05/02-01 and sells on 2026-03-01.
- impact: Anything owned before tracking started (the normal case: gold, a flat, shares typed in after the fact) cannot be sold with the real sale date or linked to the bank credit that already arrived; the message blames quantity, not the date, so the user cannot understand it.
- fix: Failing test first (SellAssetTest): lot purchasedAt = '2026-03-05', sale soldAt = '2026-03-02' linked to credit 't-in' -> expect success with held 10 g. Fix options (owner decision): (a) add an optional 'bought when?' date to AddHolding and default the opening lot to an 'owned from before' date instead of today; (b) treat the AddHolding opening lot (no transactionId) as held since the beginning in saleOutcome/computePosition; (c) at minimum show a clear message 'sale date is before the date you recorded owning it' and add a real date picker to the sell sheet.
- second reviewer: AddHolding.kt:80 always dates the opening lot today, and NewHolding (HoldingForm.kt:58) has no purchase-date field. AssetSaleGain.kt:16-21 drops lots dated after soldAt and throws ASSET_SELL_OVER_HELD with held 0, so Yesterday (AssetTradeSheet.kt:195) or a linked credit (:153 sets day = op.date) fails on an item added today. One correction: the date is not strictly forced, since the user can tap Today again at :194 and keep the link, but then the sale date is wrong. Any fix must also cover the same replay check in Assets.kt:125-142, or a saved earlier-dated sale will throw in listPortfolio.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: When you add something you already own, should the app ask 'اشتريته امتى؟' (optional), or just consider it owned from long ago so any sale date is accepted?

### todo-assets-3 - Archived assets and fully sold assets disappear from every list: cannot be un-archived, sold or deleted
- severity: high | verdict: confirmed | urgency: now | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/LoadHoldings.kt:33`
- evidence: LoadHoldings.kt:33 filters out archived assets and assets whose lots exist but heldQuantity <= 0. 'حاجاتي' and the category page are built only from this snapshot (HoldingsRoutes.kt:68,80). The old asset list (InvestmentState.kt:73 'others', which includes archived) is no longer rendered anywhere: grep for '.others' / assetLine( in ui shows only its definition. The un-archive button and the delete button exist only on AssetDetailScreen (lines 102, 126), which is reachable only from a category row, the assistant, or an operation's sale link (OperationLinksSection.kt:51). Archiving is one long-press tap with no confirmation, no toast and no undo, and errors are swallowed (HoldingsRoutes.kt:88: runCatching { archiveAsset(...) }; version++). There is no archived-assets section (ArchivedRow/ArchivedSection are used only in dues and savings goals). Scenario: long-press 'ذهب' -> 'أرشفة' -> the row vanishes; there is now no screen that lists it, so it cannot be restored, sold or deleted. Likewise after selling all 10 g the asset vanishes; a wrong sale can then only be reached through the sale's money operation link.
- impact: Data the user owns becomes unreachable; an accidental archive is effectively permanent from the UI, and a fully sold asset (with its gain/loss history) cannot be reviewed or deleted from the Investment screen.
- fix: Failing test first: a use-case/presenter test that after archiveAsset(id,true) some list returned to the Investment screen still contains the asset (e.g. HoldingsSnapshot.archived or a new LoadArchivedHoldings), and the same for heldQuantity = 0 with sales. Then add a collapsed 'مؤرشف / اتباع' section under 'حاجاتي' (reuse dues/ArchivedRow pattern) that opens AssetDetailRoute, and give the long-press archive an undo strip.
- second reviewer: LoadHoldings.kt:33 drops archived and fully sold assets, and holdings/HoldingsRoutes.kt:68 and :87 build the category page only from that snapshot. InvestmentState.kt:73 'others' has no UI consumer, and ArchivedRow/ArchivedSection exist only in dues and SavingsGoalsScreen. HoldingsRoutes.kt:88 archives with runCatching, no toast, no undo, and un-archive exists only at AssetDetailScreen.kt:102. Hiding sold-out assets is deliberate per the docstring at LoadHoldings.kt:23, but nothing in OVERRIDES.md approves them being unreachable; the only ways back are the assistant link (AssistLinks.kt:120) and the sale's operation link (OperationLinksSection.kt:51).
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Where should archived and fully sold items appear: a small folded section at the bottom of 'حاجاتي', or inside each type page?


## MEDIUM (21)

### sec-android-2 - No release signing key: the owner build is signed with the PC debug keystore and the release type is unminified and unsigned
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: not_built
- where: `native-app/androidApp/build.gradle.kts:64`
- evidence: Build type 'fast' (lines 58-67), the one given to the owner and testers, sets signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("debug") (line 64). 'release' (lines 50-52) sets isMinifyEnabled = false and has no signingConfig. No signingConfigs block exists in the file; a grep for keystore.properties / release keystore / signingConfigs across the Kotlin build scripts found only the two debug references. proguard-rules.pro:6 has -dontobfuscate. PLAN-0.6.md:28-29 (SECRETS-1) records this as deferred by the owner to just before store publishing (it cites OVERRIDES section 118-2, which I did not open). .gitignore lines 39-40 exclude *.jks and *.keystore, and no keystore is tracked.
- impact: Google Play rejects debug-signed bundles. When the real key is introduced, every existing install (owner, brother in Egypt, testers) must uninstall first, which wipes device-only data (SMS inbox queue, per-sender wallet mapping, learned SMS shapes, person photos, lock setting) and the Google sign-in SHA-1 must be re-registered. The debug keystore lives only on one PC; losing it has the same effect today. The more testers before the switch, the more painful it is.
- fix: Before adding more testers: create an upload keystore kept outside the repo (same pattern as secrets/google-services.json), read its path and passwords from an untracked properties file or environment variables, add a release signingConfig, point 'fast' (or a new 'store' type) at it, register its SHA-1/SHA-256 in Firebase, and enrol in Play App Signing. Plan the one-time reinstall message for testers.
- second reviewer: The facts hold: native-app/androidApp/build.gradle.kts:64 signs 'fast' with the debug key, 'release' (50-52) is unminified with no signingConfig, and no release keystore config exists in any native-app build script. But OVERRIDES.md:2681 (section 118-2, owner 2026-10-24) explicitly decides to stay on the test key until just before store publishing and accepts a one-time uninstall/reinstall with data returning from Firebase, so this is a recorded deferral, not an oversight. What remains is that the decision does not mention device-only data lost on reinstall or the single-PC debug keystore having no backup.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Switch to the real signing key now (before more testers) or keep the recorded decision to do it once just before the store?

### sec-android-3 - READ_SMS and RECEIVE_SMS are restricted on Google Play; no declaration plan found in the docs
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: weakened | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: not_built
- where: `native-app/device/src/androidMain/AndroidManifest.xml:4`
- evidence: Lines 4-5 request READ_SMS and RECEIVE_SMS; line 9 registers BankSmsReceiver for SMS_RECEIVED. Both are used (BankSmsReceiver.kt, AndroidBankSms/AndroidSmsInbox). A grep of OVERRIDES.md, PLAN-0.6.md, KOTLIN_PLAN.md and ARCHITECTURE.md for 'Permissions Declaration' and Arabic equivalents returned nothing. OVERRIDES.md:2520 only mentions the Data safety page for usage data.
- impact: Play only allows these permissions for default SMS handlers or approved exceptions (SMS-based money management is one), and requires a Permissions Declaration form, a demo video and a prominent in-app disclosure before the runtime prompt. Without it the upload is rejected or the app removed. This can block the store release for weeks.
- fix: Add a release checklist item: Permissions Declaration under the money-management exception, in-app disclosure screen shown before the SMS permission prompt (the code comment in MainActivity.kt:23 says the prompt comes after an explanation on the bank SMS screen; verify its wording matches Play's requirement), privacy policy URL describing SMS handling, and a fallback plan (paste/import only build) if the declaration is refused.
- second reviewer: native-app/device/src/androidMain/AndroidManifest.xml:4-5,9 does request READ_SMS/RECEIVE_SMS and register the receiver, but the claim that the docs have no declaration plan is wrong: KOTLIN_PLAN.md:83 records the 'SMS-based money management' exception with a declaration form and human review (verified 2026-09-19), and PLAN-0.6.md:543 notes the privacy policy and Data safety requirement. The remaining gap is smaller: no concrete release checklist (demo video, prominent disclosure wording check, fallback build if refused) was found in OVERRIDES/PLAN-0.6/KOTLIN_PLAN.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Confirm we apply for the SMS money-management exception and accept a paste-only fallback if refused.

### sec-android-4 - Usage analytics has no off switch or disclosure; crash reports are on by default and carry the account id
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/FirebaseUsage.kt:55`
- evidence: FirebaseUsage.create (lines 48-57) calls setAnalyticsCollectionEnabled(true) whenever BuildConfig.USAGE_ANALYTICS is true (debug and fast builds, build.gradle.kts:37) with no user preference checked; lines 36-43 grant ANALYTICS_STORAGE consent in code. CrashReports.kt:30 reads the 'enabled' preference with default true (opt-out), and line 64 sets the Firebase uid as the Crashlytics user id. This is the owner decision in OVERRIDES.md section 114 item 4 (line 2519: sending works with no explanation line and no switch) and section 90. Mitigations that are really in place: ad id permissions removed (main AndroidManifest.xml:7-9), ad/SSAID collection disabled (lines 33-37), no analytics setUserId, typed events only, CrashSanitizer strips exception messages.
- impact: The content sent looks safe, but shipping to the public with silent analytics and a uid-tagged crash stream conflicts with Play's User Data policy (privacy policy plus accurate Data safety form are mandatory) and with consent expectations under Saudi PDPL and Egyptian data law. OVERRIDES itself notes (line 2520) the Data safety page must declare it. Google signals can only be turned off in the Analytics console (OVERRIDES.md:2523), not in the app.
- fix: Before the store: privacy policy page linked from the app and the listing; one line of disclosure at first run; a single switch that controls both analytics and crash reports (analytics switch is a few lines next to the existing crash switch); confirm Google signals is off in the console; fill Data safety (crash logs, diagnostics, app interactions, user id).
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Keep section 114-4 (no switch, no notice) for testers only and add a notice plus switch before the store?

### sec-android-5 - Sign-out leaves personal data on the device: support messages, person photos, SMS sender settings, problem log
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built
- where: `native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/AndroidSignOutCleanup.kt:12`
- evidence: beforeSignOut (lines 12-16) wipes only: SMS inbox rows and repair backups (DeviceSignOut.kt:14-17) and the two per-account drafts; afterSignOut (18-20) clears the Firestore cache. Not touched: filesDir/support/outbox.log and pending.log, which hold the free text the user typed in 'send us' and are not keyed by account (FileSupportOutbox.kt:20-24, wired at AppContainer.kt:113); filesDir/person-photos/<personId>.jpg (AndroidPersonPhotos.kt:61-64); the 'sms-inbox-settings' preferences, where SmsInboxStore.forget (lines 66-69) deletes messages only and keeps sender list, per-sender wallet ids, salary answers and learned shape fingerprints; 'alert-seen' (DeviceStores.kt:13), alert learning stores, and filesDir/problems/problems.log (ProblemLogSetup.kt:24).
- impact: On a shared or handed-over phone, the next account (or anyone with device access) can still find the previous user's typed support messages, photos of the people he tracks, and which banks he uses. Pending support messages from the previous user can also be re-sent by the background cycle after another account signs in.
- fix: Extend the cleanup: delete support/ (after attempting a final send), person-photos/, problems log, and remove all keys containing the uid from sms-inbox-settings, alert-seen and the alert learning prefs. Add a unit test listing every device store so new ones cannot be forgotten.

### sec-android-6 - allowBackup=false but no dataExtractionRules: phone-to-phone transfer can still copy app data on Android 12+
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built
- where: `native-app/androidApp/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:22`
- evidence: The application tag (lines 20-27) sets android:allowBackup="false" and has no android:dataExtractionRules or android:fullBackupContent. The only res/xml file in the module is src/emulator/res/xml/emulator_network.xml. targetSdk is 36 (build.gradle.kts:29). For apps targeting API 31+, Android documents that allowBackup=false disables cloud backup but does not disable device-to-device transfer.
- impact: When the user migrates to a new phone by cable or Wi-Fi, the plain SQLite SMS inbox (sanitized message bodies), the Firestore offline cache with all transactions and balances, support text, person photos and the app-lock flag may be copied to the new device outside the app's control. Repair backups are safe (noBackupFilesDir, AndroidLocalFiles.kt:57).
- fix: Add res/xml/data_extraction_rules.xml with <cloud-backup> and <device-transfer> both excluding all domains (root, file, database, sharedpref, external), reference it with android:dataExtractionRules, and add android:fullBackupContent="false" for API 30 and below.

### todo-transfers-6 - Merging the other bank's message takes any incoming of the same amount, even a salary or a person's transfer
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:57`
- evidence: mergeIncoming() lines 49-66: 'incoming' is every SMS line on the request wallet without transferToWalletId; the match (lines 57-60) is same wallet, same direction, same amount and currency, within 1 day. No check of sms.kind, of the party, or of economicKindConfirmed; the matched line is then overwritten to confirmed INTERNAL_TRANSFER (lines 63-65) and the auto leg deleted (line 62). Scenario: auto leg +1,000,000 on wallet B from the owner's transfer; next day someone else sends 10,000 to B (or salary of the same amount arrives) before bank B's transfer message. That income is turned into an internal transfer; the real transfer message then arrives as a separate unclassified incoming. Balance stays right, but income is hidden and a pending-incoming question appears for the wrong row. Not tested.
- impact: Real income of the same amount can silently disappear from income totals.
- fix: Failing test first: auto leg on B, then an incoming SALARY-kind or named-party line of the same amount; assert it is NOT merged. Fix: only merge lines whose sms.kind is a transfer kind and whose kind is not already confirmed; prefer a line whose sender digits/name point back to the source wallet; if ambiguous, ask.

### todo-transfers-7 - Auto second leg is created before the fee split, so it carries amount plus fee when the fee is inside the SMS total
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/SmsLaneEffects.kt:36`
- evidence: OwnTransferLegEffect is added at line 36 and SmsFeeEffect at line 40. addLegs copies t.amountMinor into the leg (OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:111-116 via t.copy). Later SmsFeeEffect.split (SmsFeeEffect.kt:84-88) reduces only the main line to amount minus fee when fee.includedInAmount (set in core/SmsFees.kt:244 when the message has a total-due line). The leg in ctx.extra keeps the gross. Scenario: transfer 10,000 with fee 1.15 and a total line 10,001.15: wallet A row becomes 1,000,000 plus a fee row 115, but wallet B leg is +1,000,115. When bank B's message (1,000,000) arrives, mergeIncoming needs equal amounts (line 59) so it does not merge: B shows +2,000,115, an extra of about 10,000. Whether the owner's bank really sends a total-due line on own-account transfers was not verified against a real message. No test covers leg + fee together.
- impact: Other wallet over-credited by the fee, and the later real message is counted as a second transfer.
- fix: Failing test first: full smsRecordEffects chain on an own-transfer body with fee + total-due line; assert leg amount equals the principal and that the later incoming of the principal merges. Fix: build the leg from the principal (apply smsFeeToRecord before creating the leg, or move leg creation after SmsFeeEffect).

### todo-types-4 - Employer created from the 'this is my salary' answer has no expected amount, and 'salary' cannot be saved as a rule
- severity: medium (finder said high) | verdict: weakened | urgency: now | effort: M | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/PayerKinds.kt:54`
- evidence: salaryByPayerAllowed returns false when there is no salary word and amountNearSalary(amount, employer.expectedMinor) is false; amountNearSalary returns false for a null expected amount (lines 30-33). An employer created by IncomeAnswers.markSalary(newEmployer) goes through ManageEmployers.save, which builds IncomeSource(id, name, '', JOB, currency, start, end, createdAt) with no expectedMinor and no day (ManageEmployers.kt:69-71). When the owner then answers 'full-time salary' on a later transfer from the same account, markSalary only writes that transaction (IncomeAnswers.kt:83-87: the key is already in payerKeys, so nothing else is saved). The rule path refuses salary: PayerKindAnswers.answerAs throws unless kind is in PAYER_ASK_KINDS or OTHER_KINDS (PayerKindAnswers.kt:119), and neither list contains SALARY (PayerKinds.kt:22, IncomeAnswers.kt:147-150). Scenario: 5,000.00 from account K on 2026-09-27, owner answers 'salary, new employer' -> K linked. 5,000.00 on 2026-10-27, text 'incoming transfer' -> no word, expected null -> asksPayerKind true (PayerKinds.kt:127-133) -> 'what is this money?'. Owner picks salary again -> one row. 2026-11-27: same. Also with an expected amount of 8,000.00, a month of 9,150.00 (overtime, +14%) is outside the 10% tolerance (PayerKinds.kt:15) and asks. This contradicts the owner's recorded answer in OVERRIDES section 64 ('any transfer from it is recorded as salary by itself'), later narrowed by PLAN-0.6 section 7 / OVERRIDES 108. Test: PayerKindsFlowTest sets expectedMinor explicitly in its fixture (line 66); the null-amount case is not covered there.
- impact: For anyone paid from a personal or company account whose bank text does not contain the word salary, the app asks about the salary every month and there is no way to tell it 'this account always pays my salary'.
- fix: Failing test first: employer created via markSalary(newEmployer) on deposit 1; record deposit 2 (same account, same amount, next month, no salary word); assert it is confirmed SALARY. Fix options (owner to pick): (a) when the owner answers 'salary' on a transfer from an already-linked account, save a payer rule of kind SALARY (allow SALARY in setPayerRule and in applyKnownPayerSalary's rule branch); (b) when an employer is created from a salary answer, pre-fill expectedMinor from that transaction and ask for the day; (c) treat a missing expected amount as 'any amount near payday'.
- second reviewer: The code facts hold: amountNearSalary returns false for null (PayerKinds.kt:31), ManageEmployers.save builds the employer with no amount or day (lines 69-71), markSalary writes only the one row when the key is already linked (IncomeAnswers.kt:83-87) and answerAs rejects SALARY (PayerKindAnswers.kt:119). But this is not a hidden bug: OVERRIDES.md:2431 item (b) lists exactly 'employer without a written salary is asked unless the text says salary' as an open question waiting for the owner, line 2444 records the owner-approved design that removed 'salary' from the question, and §108 (line 2425) explicitly cancels the §64 'any transfer is salary' answer, so the claimed contradiction is already resolved in the file. 'No way to tell it' is overstated since the amount can be added through the source edit sheet (IncomeSourceDetailScreen.kt:108), though that path only works for a monthly job once todo-types-3 is fixed; the fix options must stay an owner question.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: بعد ما تأكد إن حساب معين بيحوّلك المرتب: أي تحويل منه يتسجل مرتب لوحده (قرارك القديم)، ولا بس اللي مبلغه وميعاده قريبين من المرتب (القرار الأحدث)، ولا نضيف اختيار «ده مرتب دايمًا من الحساب ده»؟

### todo-types-5 - 'Company or entity' answer stores the chosen kind on the sender but never applies it
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/TransferParties.kt:263`
- evidence: markCompany saves TransferParty(verdict = COMPANY, kind = kind) (TransferPartyAnswers.kt:83) but applyTransferVerdict returns the transaction unchanged for COMPANY (line 263); party.kind is read only in the LABEL branch (line 268). The enum comment says each amount is asked separately and kind is 'the last choice' (line 22). A grep for party.kind in ui and app found no reader that pre-selects it. Scenario: 230.00 refund from an insurance company on 2026-10-05, owner picks 'company' + 'refund'; 180.00 from the same sender on 2026-11-02 arrives UNCLASSIFIED and is asked again with nothing pre-selected. The test TransferPartyAnswersTest.kt:80 asserts exactly this ('the next one from the entity is asked about its kind').
- impact: Recurring payers that are not employers (insurance, a platform, a landlord's office) generate the same question on every transfer; the stored 'last choice' gives the owner no benefit.
- fix: Failing test: after markCompany(REFUND), record a second incoming transfer from the same key and assert the card proposes REFUND as the first/pre-selected choice (or confirms it, per owner decision). Minimal fix: surface party.kind as the suggested answer with one-tap confirm; fuller fix: an opt-in 'always this type from this entity' that makes COMPANY behave like LABEL.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: الجهة أو الشركة اللي بتحوّلك أكتر من مرة: تحب البرنامج يحط آخر نوع اخترته لوحده، ولا يقترحه عليك وتأكد بضغطة، ولا يسأل من الأول كل مرة؟

### todo-types-6 - 'Make it a rule' box in 'what is this money?' starts unticked, so the default path asks again
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/ui/screens/operations/MoneyWhatCard.kt:63`
- evidence: var rule by remember(transactionId) { mutableStateOf(false) } and the value is passed to payer.answer / answerCustom / answerRefund (lines 156-163). PayerKindAnswers.answerAs returns before saving a rule when makeRule is false (PayerKindAnswers.kt:131); its doc says 'otherwise he will be asked again next time' (line 89). This is the owner's own change recorded in HANDOVER line 762 and OVERRIDES section 108 (r06-1b3). The state is keyed by transaction, so the tick is never remembered between questions either. Scenario: employer account sends 300.00 expense reimbursements on 2026-10-10 and 2026-10-24; owner picks 'reimbursement' and saves without ticking; both ask, and so will the next.
- impact: The deliberate default produces exactly the symptom the owner later reported. He probably does not connect the small unticked box with the repeated question.
- fix: No code change without the owner. Options: tick the box by default; or after the second identical answer from the same account show 'you chose this last time too - remember it?'; or keep the text line under the box more explicit. A test for the chosen behaviour should extend MoneyWhatFlowTest.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: علامة «خليها قاعدة للمرات الجاية» تبدأ متعلّمة لوحدها، ولا تفضل فاضية زي ما طلبت قبل كده، ولا البرنامج يعرضها عليك بعد تاني مرة تختار نفس الإجابة؟

### todo-types-7 - Client, rent, investment, pension and 'other' income sources can never be linked to any credit
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: L | status: not_built
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/ManageEmployers.kt:162`
- evidence: Every path that links a sender account to a source goes through ManageEmployers.find, which requires isEmployer(source) (line 162), i.e. kind JOB or PART_TIME only (IncomeSources.kt:95, Employers.kt:44). The automatic question 'is this a salary from ...?' only considers salaried kinds (IncomeSignals.kt:19-20, 52). attributedSourceId (IncomeSignals.kt:27-35) relies on payerKeys, which therefore stay empty for the other kinds. The detail page shows 'payer: not asked' for them (IncomeSourceDetailScreen.kt:113-114). PENSION is documented as 'counted as salaried' (IncomeSources.kt:21) but is not in SALARIED_KINDS. The hub shows all seven kinds as equal tiles (IncomeHubScreen.kt:71-78). PLAN-0.6 lines 123 and 171 record the owner's request to link a transfer to a property's rent with a paid/unpaid month table - marked as needing design.
- impact: Five of the seven tiles are name-only records: a freelancer's client or a rented flat shows no money, cannot be matched to its transfers, and its transfers keep arriving as unanswered questions.
- fix: Failing test: a CLIENT source with a linked sender key; record an incoming transfer from that key; assert attributedSourceId returns the source and the kind is FREELANCE (or the source's mapped kind). Fix: generalise attach/detach to all kinds with a kind-to-EconomicKind map (client -> freelance, rent -> rental income, pension -> benefit/salary per owner), and add the missing 'is this from ...?' question for them. Until then, say so on the tile instead of looking complete.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: العميل والإيجار والمعاش وعائد الاستثمار: تحب نربط بيهم التحويلات زي جهة الشغل بالظبط دلوقتي، ولا نستنى تصميمك لجدول الإيجار الأول؟

### todo-types-8 - Late-salary alert ignores the profile payday: a job without its own day never alerts
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/IncomeSignals.kt:125`
- evidence: lateIncomeCandidates returns null for a monthly source when s.expectedDayOfMonth is null (line 125) and IncomeSourceSignals.alertCandidates exits early when no source has expectedDayOfMonth (IncomeSourceSignals.kt:127). Neither falls back to the profile payday, although the model states that a monthly job's day is the profile payday (IncomeSources.kt:109) and salaryByPayerAllowed does fall back (PayerKinds.kt:56). Employers created from the employer sheet or the salary answer never get a day (ManageEmployers.kt:69-71). Scenario: owner's job, payday 28 in the profile, sender linked, day field on the source empty; no salary by 2026-11-01 (28 + 3 days grace) -> no alert. I did not open the alert engine's tests, so coverage is unconfirmed.
- impact: The 'your salary is late' alert silently never appears for the most common setup (one job, payday in the profile).
- fix: Failing test: source with payerKeys, expectedDayOfMonth = null, profile payday 28, today 2026-11-01, no attributed deposit since 2026-10-13 -> expect one INCOME_LATE candidate. Fix: pass the profile payday into lateIncomeCandidates and use expectedDayOfMonth ?: payday for JOB sources.

### todo-types-9 - Archiving a running income source closes it with today's date without asking (OVERRIDES 119-2)
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: not_built
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/ArchiveDues.kt:56`
- evidence: archivedSource sets endedAt = maxOf(today, s.startedAt) and endedByArchive = true when the source has no end date (line 56); EntityArchiver.setArchived takes only (id, archived) (line 16), so no date can be supplied. OVERRIDES section 119 item 2 (line 2728) says the app must ask 'when did this job end?' with 'today' pre-selected and 'pick a day' beside it, and the section heading says it is for the next round. Scenario: owner left a job on 2026-08-31 and archives it on 2026-11-14 -> the period is stored as ending 2026-11-14; a transfer from the old employer's account on 2026-10-10 is inside the period, so employerByPayer (Employers.kt:62) still matches it and it can be confirmed as salary automatically, and the before/after comparison and timeline show the wrong period.
- impact: Wrong end dates on past jobs, and money from an old employer after he left (for example an end-of-service payment) can be auto-labelled as that job's salary.
- fix: Failing test: archive a running source with an explicit end date 2026-08-31 and assert endedAt == 2026-08-31, endedByArchive == true, and that restore reopens it. Fix: add an optional endedAt parameter to the income-source archiver, and a small sheet (today / pick a day) before archiving a source that has no end date.

### todo-types-10 - Category memory works for shops only and for future transactions only
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: later | effort: M | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/MerchantMemory.kt:19`
- evidence: The class doc states the last choice wins and affects the next transactions only - old ones are not re-categorised (line 19) - and that names which are not shops are never remembered: generic operation titles, transfer-party names (a person or an account) and confirmed internal transfers (lines 21-24). EditTransaction.setCategory calls merchantMemory.remember for the one pick (EditTransaction.kt:102) and RememberChosenCategoryEffect does it at record time only for owner-chosen lines (RememberChosenCategoryEffect.kt:12). The background wiring does include it (BackgroundWiring.kt:47). Scenario: owner sets category 'rent' on a 2,000.00 transfer to his landlord; next month's transfer to the same person arrives with no category. Or: he fixes the category of one coffee-shop purchase; the eight older purchases from the same shop stay as they were until he runs 'apply rules to previous' in the rules screen (RulesCards.kt:59).
- impact: For transfers, a category choice is never reused; for shops, the owner sees old rows still uncategorised and may read that as 'not saved'.
- fix: After a manual category pick on a shop, offer a one-line 'apply to the N earlier ones from this shop?' (preview count, undo). For transfers, tie the category to the sender decision together with the kind memory proposed in todo-types-1.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: لما تغيّر تصنيف عملية من محل: تحب البرنامج يسألك «أطبّقه على العمليات القديمة من نفس المحل؟»

### todo-assets-4 - Row long-press menu offers only open/archive; delete parameter exists but is never passed
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built

- evidence: AssetCategoryScreen.kt:140 calls assetQuickItems(false, { a.open(row) }, { a.archive(row) }) with no delete argument, although InvestmentScreen.kt:216 defines assetQuickItems(..., delete: (() -> Unit)? = null) and builds the menu with canDelete = delete != null. No sell action exists in the quick menu at all. So from the list the only destructive-looking action is 'أرشفة', which leads to the dead-end in todo-assets-3.

### todo-assets-5 - Real estate / company / personal / other kinds are stored as quantity 1 unit, and the sell sheet requires a typed quantity with no prefill or 'sell all'
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: partially_built
- where: `native-app/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/ui/screens/investment/AssetTradeState.kt:68`
- evidence: HoldingForm.kt:126,134,141 create these holdings with quantity = QUANTITY_SCALE (one unit). tradeRequest (AssetTradeState.kt:68) returns Invalid(ASSET_TRADE_ERR_QTY) when qty is blank; TradeFields (AssetTradeSheet.kt:200) shows an empty quantity field with placeholder 0 and the unit label 'وحدة'. There is no default to the held quantity and no partial-sale concept for a flat. Scenario: owner sells his flat for 500,000: leaves quantity empty -> 'اكتب الكمية'; types the area 120 -> 'selling 120, held 1'. Also these kinds are created with costKnown=false and a zero-cost lot (AddHolding.kt:66-80), so the gain card shows 'not available' (by design, OVERRIDES 117-2). No test covers selling a QUANTITY_SCALE holding from the form.
- impact: Selling a flat, a company share or a personal item looks broken unless the user guesses he must type 1.
- fix: Failing test first (tradeRequest): mode SELL, blank qty, asset held = QUANTITY_SCALE with unit-type holding -> expect a Sell request for the whole holding. Then prefill qty with the held quantity (or hide the field and sell all) for holding types whose quantity is always one unit, and add a 'كله' chip for metals/shares.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: For a flat or a personal item, is selling always 'all of it' (no quantity question)?

### todo-assets-6 - Sale linked to a bank credit records gain from the typed amount, not from the money that actually arrived
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/SellAsset.kt:116`
- evidence: SellAsset.sell with linkTransactionId (lines 116-121) only checks isCandidate (same wallet, incoming, not used); it never compares the credit's amountMinor with the sale's net. matchingCredits (line 93) lists credits within 10% of net (near, line 163), so a different amount is expected. The form only copies the credit amount when the amount field is blank (AssetTradeSheet.kt:153). Gain is computed from the typed gross (line 107 preview). Scenario (same numbers as HANDOVER line 322): 10 shares cost 1,000.00; sell 4 for typed 600.00, link the bank credit of 555.00, no fee typed -> stored gain = 600 - 400 = +200.00, but the bank only received 555.00 (real gain 155.00). The wallet balance stays right (no new transaction), the realized gain is overstated by 45.00. SellAssetTest.linkingToTheCreditThatArrivedAddsNoDuplicate uses a matching amount only.
- impact: Realized gain/loss on the asset page and in the portfolio total can be wrong by up to 10% of the sale whenever the typed proceeds differ from the linked credit.
- fix: Failing test first: sale gross 60,000 minor, linked credit 55,500, no fee -> expect either a rejection asking to explain the difference or an auto fee of 4,500 so gain = 15,500. Fix per owner decision: treat the difference as fees, or force gross = credit amount when linked.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: When the linked transfer is smaller than the sale price you typed, should the difference be recorded automatically as fees?

### todo-assets-7 - No way to correct or remove a single purchase or sale; the only fix for a wrong entry is deleting the whole asset
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: M | status: not_built
- where: `native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/ManageAssets.kt:196`
- evidence: ManageAssets (whole file read, 231 lines) exposes addAsset, linkToFeed, archiveAsset, recordPurchase, recordSale, setPrice only; a repo-wide grep for deleteSale/removeSale/removeLot/deleteLot/editSale in native-app found no use case. SellAsset has no undo. So a sale typed with the wrong quantity or amount (or a duplicated purchase) stays forever unless the asset is deleted with RemoveAsset, which removes every lot and sale (RemoveMoneyEntities.kt:82-83). Deleting the cash transaction created by the sale from the operations screen was not traced (see notChecked).
- impact: A typo in a sale permanently distorts held quantity, cost basis and realized gain; users will read this as 'I cannot delete it'.
- fix: Add RemoveAssetTrade (lot or sale) with the same undo pattern and the same 'no sale may exceed holdings after removal' replay check via computePosition; failing test: record sale of 4 g, remove it, expect held back to 15 g and the created ASSET_SELL transaction removed.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Should each line in 'المشتريات والمبيعات' be deletable/editable on its own?

### todo-assets-8 - Sell/save and asset actions catch only IllegalArgument/IllegalState; a storage failure escapes the coroutine
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_suspected
- where: `native-app/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/ui/screens/investment/AssetTradeSheet.kt:110`
- evidence: AssetTradeSheet.save (lines 95-116) wraps deps.sell.sell / recordPurchase / setPrice in try with catch IllegalArgumentException and IllegalStateException only, inside scope.launch from rememberCoroutineScope. AssetDetailScreen.act (line 77) does the same. Any other exception from the repositories (network/permission/serialization) is not caught there. By contrast DeleteWithChoices.kt:82,112 catches Exception and shows a toast. SellAsset.sell also performs three separate writes (AddTransaction line 124, recordSale line 132, linked txn update line 139); only the first is compensated (line 134). If the final update fails, the sale is saved and linked but the bank credit keeps its old kind (still counted as income).
- impact: A storage error during sell could crash the screen or leave a half-recorded sale instead of showing a message; I could not confirm which exception types the Firestore adapter throws.
- fix: Catch Exception (rethrow CancellationException) like DeleteWithChoices and show the message; run the sale + linked-transaction patch in repos.uow. Failing test: fake TransactionRepository.update throws -> expect no sale stored.

### todo-ui-6 - Notification permission: 'asked' flag is saved before asking and nothing in the app can ask again
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: before_release | effort: S | status: bug_root_cause_found
- where: `native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/MainActivity.kt:143`
- evidence: MainActivity.kt:139-145: `if (prefs.getBoolean(ASKED_NOTIFICATIONS, false)) return` ... `prefs.edit().putBoolean(ASKED_NOTIFICATIONS, true).apply()` then `permissions.requestNotifications()`. The flag is written first, so if the process dies or the activity is recreated while the dialog is up, or the user dismisses it, the app never asks again. Grep for requestNotifications across native-app finds only the interface (ui/app/Session.kt:42), the Android implementation and this one call. device/.../AndroidDeviceNotifier.kt:36 and :73 document that without the permission nothing is shown (NoticeOutcome.BLOCKED). No test references this path.
- impact: On Android 13+ a single dismissal means auto-recorded bank messages and due reminders never notify, and the user has no in-app switch to fix it.
- fix: Write the flag after the result; keep 'denied' separately; add a row in Bank SMS settings (and the alerts screen) that calls requestNotifications() or opens system notification settings when the system no longer shows the dialog. Test first: a small pure policy function shouldAskNotifications(asked, granted, deniedCount) with cases for dismissed/denied/granted.

### sync-8 - Two-device edits are last-write-wins on the whole entity, and a stale save recreates a document deleted on the other phone
- severity: medium | verdict: unverified | urgency: later | effort: L | status: by_design
- where: `native-app/firestore/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/firestore/FirestoreSpace.kt:105`
- evidence: saveAll writes every known field of the entity with set(..., merge = true) plus explicit deletes for empty optionals (mergeForm, lines 92-96, and line 105). There is no version, updatedAt precondition or tombstone. A set-merge on a document that no longer exists creates it again with all fields. Field-level update() exists only for the transaction patch path (FirestoreTransactionRepository.kt:45-78). Use cases such as DeleteTransaction save whole entities (DeleteTransaction.kt:123, 134-142).
- impact: Phone A offline changes one thing on a transaction (or on a wallet, debt, recurring item) while phone B changes or deletes it; when A reconnects its queued full-entity write overwrites B's change or brings the deleted row back, which changes the balance. No one is told. Balances themselves are derived from rows, so there is no lost-update on a counter, but the rows can be wrong.
- fix: Short term: route single-field edits through update() patches (which fail instead of resurrecting) and surface the failure (sync-5). Longer term: add an updatedAt/revision field checked in the mirror before applying a queued full-entity write, and keep a small tombstone list for deleted ids.
- OWNER DECISION NEEDED: Is silent last-write-wins between two phones acceptable for the first public release, or should conflicting edits be detected?

## Refuted by the second reviewer (do not act on)

- sec-firebase-3: File/CSV import stores the raw statement line and description in Firestore without redaction (last-four-digits rule not applied) - The write layer already redacts: data/StoreRules.kt:23-32 storeForm replaces any run of 5+ digits (and 12+ digits grouped by spaces or dashes) with ****last4 in every top-level string of transactions, sourceRecords and importBatches. Every save goes through it (DocCodec.kt:29 toStore, FirestoreSpace.kt:93), so rawLine, rawDescription and rawMerchantName from CSV are not stored in full. The proposed parse-time redactSms was already tried and removed because it broke transfer-party detection (HANDOVER.md:285, PRIVACY-2). The only residue is short grouped numbers under 12 digits, such as a spaced phone number.

## Per lane: summary, strengths, not checked

### sec-android
Android device security/privacy lane, branch r051-int (0.5.5). Read all 6 manifests, the app build script, R8 rules, MainActivity/Application/AppContainer, lock, SMS receiver + inbox store, notifier, background worker, crash/analytics/problem-log/support-outbox, sign-out cleanup. Nothing critical found. The basics are solid: one exported launcher activity, SMS receiver protected by BROADCAST_SMS, allowBackup=false, cleartext limited to 10.0.2.2 in emulator build types only, immutable PendingIntents, lock-screen-safe notification text, sanitized crash reports, no SMS text in logs. Main gaps are release readiness (no release signing key, Play SMS-permission declaration, telemetry with no off switch or disclosure), incomplete sign-out cleanup, and no device-transfer exclusion rules. T8 (screenshots): the code blocks screenshots for the whole app whenever app lock is on; no owner decision in OVERRIDES covers this.

**Strengths:**
- Minimal attack surface: one exported launcher activity with only MAIN/LAUNCHER, the SMS receiver requires BROADCAST_SMS, ad-id permissions are removed, allowBackup=false (native-app/androidApp/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:5-48, native-app/device/src/androidMain/AndroidManifest.xml:9)
- Cleartext cannot leak into owner builds: the network security config permits HTTP only for 10.0.2.2 and is attached only to the emulator and fastEmulator source sets; the perf_uid sign-in extra is dead code outside emulator builds (native-app/androidApp/src/emulator/res/xml/emulator_network.xml, build.gradle.kts:82-85, AppContainer.kt:140-145)
- Notifications are lock-screen safe: generic text only, isLockSafe re-check at display time, VISIBILITY_PRIVATE with a public version, PendingIntent FLAG_IMMUTABLE (native-app/device/src/androidMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/device/AndroidDeviceNotifier.kt:100-132)
- SMS is sanitized before it touches disk: OTP/promo messages dropped, account and card numbers masked to last four, body blanked after processing, and no SMS text is ever logged (native-app/device/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/device/SmsSafety.kt:63-89, SmsInboxStore.kt:172-183 and 206-219, BankSmsReceiver.kt:26-28)
- Crash reporting is privacy-aware: every exception goes through CrashSanitizer (type name and stack only, no messages), only three fixed custom keys, uid cleared on sign-out, collection disabled in the manifest until the app decides (native-app/device/src/jvmShared/kotlin/app/masroufy/device/CrashSanitizer.kt:17-26, native-app/androidApp/src/main/kotlin/app/masroufy/android/CrashReports.kt:42-73)

**Not checked:**
- Whether the lock overlay fully hides content on Android below 12 (blur is a no-op there): MasroufyApp.kt:98-101 mentions a blur backdrop; the LockScreen file was not found at the CODEMAP path and I did not open the backdrop implementation. With FLAG_SECURE the recents thumbnail is covered, but someone holding the locked phone might still read numbers behind the lock sheet on old Android.
- OVERRIDES section 118-2 (signing key deferral) and section 90 text were not opened; cited via PLAN-0.6.md:29 and code comments only.
- Whether the support outbox list (FileSupportOutbox.all) is displayed in the UI to a different account after sign-in.
- Content of UsageEvents/UsageValue in core (that no amounts or merchant names can reach analytics) - relied on the OVERRIDES 114-5 description and the typed port; not read.
- FileProblemLog and RecordProblem details map contents (assumed code + error kind + counters per ProblemLogSetup.kt:38).
- AndroidBankSms / AndroidSmsInbox (on-demand READ_SMS query): whether it reads only enabled senders.
- firestore.rules for inbox/{id} and FirestoreFeedbackInbox payload - other lane.
- PLAN-0.6.md:11 states FIREBASE.md in the public repo contains the full web Firebase config including an API key; I did not open FIREBASE.md.
- The device and firestore androidInstrumentedTest manifests were not opened (test-only).
- Merged manifest of the final APK (permissions added by libraries such as WorkManager, Play services, Crashlytics) - cannot be seen without a build.
- android/ (old Capacitor app) and gradle.properties files were not read for secrets; git ls-files shows no tracked keystore, google-services.json or .env (only .env.example).
- Nothing was run on a device; PLAN-0.6 itself says ANDROID-3 and AUTH-4 were never tried on a real phone.

**Other titles:**
- firebase-trial app manifest has usesCleartextTraffic=true and an exported launcher (native-app/firebase-trial/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:5) - separate trial app, must never be distributed
- lint checkReleaseBuilds=false disables release lint including security checks (native-app/androidApp/build.gradle.kts:98)
- App Check is off by default (build.gradle.kts:20) - already tracked as AUTH-1 in PLAN-0.6
- App lock auto-disables when the phone has no screen lock (AppLock.kt:68-73) - owner decision OVERRIDES section 21, but it also silently removes the screenshot/recents protection
- R8 runs with -dontobfuscate (proguard-rules.pro:6) - class names stay readable in the APK; deliberate, low risk
- ARCHITECTURE.md:1044 still says there is no FLAG_SECURE - stale after ANDROID-3
- FLAG_SECURE is applied in onPause after the user enables the lock mid-session (MainActivity.kt:107-110); may be too late for that first recents snapshot on some devices

### todo-transfers
Transfers lane (T9, T11, T12), traced by reading code only (no build, no tests run). Own-account transfers are modelled two ways: a manual transfer is ONE row with transferToWalletId, an SMS transfer is TWO rows (legs), glued by OwnAccountByLast4Effect -> OwnTransferLegEffect (auto "#own-leg") -> OwnTransferManualMerge. Matching everywhere is only "same amount + same currency + within 1 day + right wallet/direction"; there is no check of message kind, party, or how many transfers exist. Confirmed in code: (1) T11: an incoming message recognised as "from my other account" never debits the other wallet (addLegs handles OUT lines only), so 1,000,000 minor units appear as extra money in the total; (2) a manual bank-to-cash transfer followed by the ATM SMS credits cash twice; (3) two equal transfers within a day produce only one auto leg; (4) the manual merge can swallow an unrelated purchase or incoming of the same amount; (5) the real Al Rajhi shape "to: NAME" with no digits is never recognised as own account and the zone decision creates no second leg. The T12 tests use an invented body with SmsKind.OTHER and call OwnTransferLegEffect alone, so they do not exercise the real effect chain. Which of the T11 paths the owner actually hit on his phone cannot be decided from code alone.

**Strengths:**
- A named party whose last 4 digits equal the owner's other wallet is NOT auto-confirmed as own transfer; it is asked once instead (native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt:46 and the S1 note in its header)
- Direction and wallet of a transfer come from the message's own from/to lines, with an explicit 'is this account yours?' wait state instead of guessing (native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/SmsAccountLegs.kt:32-51)
- Manual internal transfer is refused without a target wallet, with the same wallet, or across currencies, so money cannot vanish from a hand entry (native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/AddTransaction.kt:79-88)
- Unconfirmed incoming money is kept out of income as 'pending' instead of being guessed as salary (native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/EstimatedKinds.kt:80-85)
- The merge tests assert the real invariant (each wallet moves once and the total is unchanged) rather than row counts only (native-app/app/src/jvmTest/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/OwnTransferManualMergeTest.kt:62-69)

**Not checked:**
- Which T11 path the owner actually hit on his phone (incoming-only, cash doubled, or fee-inclusive leg): needs his real message text and the rows in his data
- Whether Firestore reads inside the import unit of work see rows written earlier in the same unit; if not, the two legs of one 'between your accounts' message recorded in the same pass could each create or miss an auto leg
- Whether an automatic leg later merges with a bank STATEMENT row on the same wallet through the cross-source dedupe (CrossSourcePreview.kt:112-124 was read, the matching rule itself was not), or is counted twice
- Whether BackgroundWiring and the screen lane both pass txns into smsRecordEffects (only the first lines of the call at wiring/BackgroundWiring.kt:44 were seen)
- How an unclassified OUTGOING transfer is estimated in spending and budgets (EstimatedKinds.kt was read only to line 90); the budget text 'زيادة {0}' (TextsAreaBudgets.kt:56) could also be what the owner saw if the transfer was counted as spending
- Egyptian message shapes and the cross-country pair flow (TransferBetweenSpaces) were not traced
- UI screens (TransfersScreen, BalanceWhyScreen, add-operation sheet) were not opened; only use cases and core
- No test was run; test coverage statements come from reading OwnTransferLegEffectTest.kt and OwnTransferManualMergeTest.kt only
- RepairOldSms / CoveredSmsRepair behaviour on old transfer rows was not traced

**Other titles:**
- Own transfer between two wallets of different currencies in the same country has no path: auto leg requires equal currency (OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:86) and manual entry rejects it (AddTransaction.kt:85)
- Undo of the other bank's batch after a merge does not restore the automatic leg (documented warning, OwnTransferLegEffect.kt:33; still an open owner question in HANDOVER)
- A party given the 'something else' label confirms incoming rows as other income before effects run (TransferParties.kt:265-270), which blocks the manual merge (OwnTransferManualMerge.kt:37)
- mergeManualIntoSms and manualOntoLegs take firstOrNull when several candidates match (OwnTransferManualMerge.kt:55, 79-80) instead of asking
- smsRecordEffects has txns = null by default (SmsLaneEffects.kt:29): any lane wired without it silently loses manual merge and message merge

### todo-types
Lane: owner to-do T10 (answers not remembered) and T7 (income sources). Read-only trace of the Kotlin code on r051-int (0.5.5).

T10 - root cause found. The owner's answer IS saved, but only on the single transaction he answered (it survives restart and sign-out because it is a Firestore field on that transaction). What is not saved is any generalisation, so the next similar transaction is asked again. Of the nine type/category flows I traced, only four create a memory that the SMS background reader applies: category per shop (MerchantMemory), "other (I write it)" on a sender (LABEL verdict), "always pays dues" on a person, and the opt-in rule on an employer account. The other five write one transaction only: review queue and the type sheet (SetEconomicKind.setOne), "extra work / sale / other type" (IncomeAnswers.markIncome), "company or entity" (kind is stored on the sender and never read back), outgoing transfer to a person ("loan or support?" every time, owner decision 75-5), and "what is this money?" when the rule box is left unticked (unticked by default, owner decision 108). On top of that there are two real bugs in the salary path: the record-time call does not pass the profile payday (RecordLines.kt:94), and an employer created through the quick "this is my salary" answer has no expected amount, so every later salary without the word "salary" in the bank text is asked again, and "salary" cannot be saved as a rule.

T7 - partially built. Creating, editing, closing, changing job, archive/restore, delete, employer accounts and the before/after comparison exist and are wired. Missing or broken: the source page does not list its own salaries (placeholder line), there is no expected-vs-received anywhere, five of the seven source kinds (client, rent, investment, pension, other) can never be linked to any credit, the late-salary alert ignores the profile payday (day 28), archiving closes the job with today's date without asking (decision 119-2 not built), and the same employer can be created through two different forms that store different fields.

Nothing was built, run or modified. About 45 tool calls used.

**Strengths:**
- Every answer has an exact undo that restores the transaction, the linked account, the rule and the note: native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/IncomeAnswers.kt:104-117 and TransferPartyAnswers.kt:166-175.
- A type the owner confirmed is never overwritten by any automatic rule: applyKnownPayerSalary returns early on economicKindConfirmed (native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/IncomeSignals.kt:72) and applyTransferVerdict does the same for PERSON and LABEL (TransferParties.kt:256, 266).
- The memories that do exist are applied by the background SMS reader, not only on screen: merchant category memory and person rules are wired as record-time effects in native-app/wiring/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/wiring/BackgroundWiring.kt:47-50, and the same prepareRecordedLines order is shared by import and SMS (RecordLines.kt:70-102).
- Money comparisons stay in integer minor units: the 10 percent salary tolerance is abs(diff) * 100 <= expected * 10 (native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/PayerKinds.kt:30-33), and senders are keyed by name plus last four digits only (TransferParties.kt:8-9, Employers.kt:11-12).
- The source page says 'not available yet' instead of showing an empty list or a zero where it has no data (native-app/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/ui/screens/more/IncomeSourceDetailScreen.kt:94-95, 147-150), and incoming money of unknown type is kept out of income rather than guessed (EstimatedKinds.kt:80-85).

**Not checked:**
- Whether transfer-party decisions (own account / person / company / label) are stored per country or per account: FirestoreContainer.kt:73 builds the repository from 'space' but I did not open FirestoreSpace, so the 'second country' behaviour of these answers is unconfirmed.
- EmployerRetroApply.kt and the rest of salaryRetroPlan (Employers.kt after line 135): whether 'apply to previous' respects the salary-like conditions or turns every unconfirmed deposit from the account into salary.
- The SMS inbox review screen (ReviewSmsInbox.kt, SmsSalaryEffect.kt, SmsLaneEffects.kt) was not opened: the 'is this your salary?' answer on a message and how it is remembered per sender is untraced.
- AnswerTransferAsks.kt, BnplTypes.kt, DueLinkAskSource.kt, RefundAsks.kt, DuplicateChargeAsks.kt, ForeignSmsAsks.kt, OwnAccountByLast4Effect.kt: these ask flows were identified from CODEMAP only and not read.
- PurchaseAsk.kt (category pop-up after choosing 'purchase') and the category picker in OperationDetail: I assume they call EditTransaction.setCategory but did not open them.
- Manual entry (AddTransaction): whether it runs applyTransferVerdict / applyKnownPayerSalary / merchant memory was not traced.
- ManageRules.kt and the 'apply rules to previous' card: not read; only its existence was seen (RulesCards.kt:59).
- core rememberableMerchantName and rememberMerchant: behaviour taken from the MerchantMemory doc comment, not from the functions themselves.
- Test coverage statements are from grep counts and a few matched lines in PayerKindsFlowTest.kt and TransferPartyAnswersTest.kt; I did not read the test bodies, and no test was run.

**Other titles:**

- The employers screen is reachable only from inside the full-time / part-time kind page (IncomeHubScreen.kt:162)

### todo-assets
T5 (cannot sell or delete an asset): both branches ARE merged into r051-int (git merge-base --is-ancestor r06-sell r051-int = 0, r06-delete = 0; 0 commits missing each). The Sell and Delete buttons are wired and unconditional for every asset kind, but only on the asset detail page (Investment > group tile > category page > row > detail; delete is the last item on that page). The use cases do not fail silently in the main path, but I found concrete dead-ends in the code that reproduce the owner's symptom: (1) a sale dated before the day the asset was typed into the app is rejected as 'selling more than you hold (0)', and the link-to-bank-credit option forces exactly such a date; (2) an archived asset (one long-press tap, no undo) and a fully sold asset vanish from every list, so they can never be un-archived, sold or deleted; (3) the long-press menu on an asset row offers only open/archive; (4) real estate/other kinds are stored as quantity '1 unit' and the sell sheet demands a typed quantity with no prefill; (5) no single purchase/sale can be corrected or removed. Asset maths itself (weighted-average cost share, integer minor units, rounding once, full-sale takes all remaining cost) is correct and tested. I did not run anything; all findings are from reading code.

**Strengths:**
- Sale maths is integer-only with one documented rounding rule and a full-sale-takes-all-remaining-cost guarantee, shared by preview and storage (native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/AssetSaleGain.kt; tested in app/src/jvmTest/.../SellAssetTest.kt:66-83)
- All validation (quantity, fees over proceeds, selling more than held) runs before any write, and the created cash transaction is rolled back if recording the sale fails (native-app/app/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/usecase/SellAsset.kt:106-136)
- Unknown cost is never shown as a number: gain is 'not available' and is not stored when costKnown=false (AssetSaleGain.kt:10, SellAsset.kt:132, ui/.../AssetSellParts.kt:147-155, ManageAssets.kt:133-136)
- Asset delete has three choices with an exact undo that restores asset, lots, sales and transactions, and counts only real linked transactions (RemoveMoneyEntities.kt:61-95; RemoveAssetTest.kt)
- Linking a sale to an existing bank credit creates no duplicate money movement and prevents a transaction being linked twice (SellAsset.kt:147-153; SellAssetTest.linkingToTheCreditThatArrivedAddsNoDuplicate)

**Not checked:**
- Nothing was built or run; the owner's actual phone build was not verified to contain r06-sell/r06-delete (only that branch r051-int contains them)
- Exception types thrown by the Firestore adapters (whether a failed write surfaces as IllegalStateException or something else) - finding todo-assets-8 depends on it
- TradeLog composable in AssetDetailScreen.kt (lines 190+) was not read; I relied on the absence of any delete-sale/lot use case
- What happens when the user deletes, from the operations screen, the cash transaction created by a sale (RevertLinkDeps includes assetSales but the revert path was not read)
- OpsKit.keep / erase / restore internals (backup, source records, wallet balance effect of erasing linked bank transactions)
- Whether orphan zakat facts or prices of a deleted asset are read by ZakatHoldings or other calculations
- Price feed missing path for stocks/funds beyond what LoadHoldings and assetDetailUi show (PreviewHoldingValue, sync of feed prices) and the real-estate projection value maths
- Firestore mapping of AssetSale.toWalletId/gainMinor and Asset.costKnown (FirestoreAssetProjectDuesRepositories.kt not opened)
- The exact text of OVERRIDES 112-4 and 118-1 main paragraphs (only later amendment lines were located by grep)
- iOS behaviour and the two-country 'elsewhere' add path

**Other titles:**
- Asset created without cost (costKnown=false) stays 'gain not available' forever even after a real purchase is recorded later (ManageAssets.recordPurchase never updates costKnown)
- Average cost per unit tile is hard-coded to 'not available' (AssetDetailScreen.kt:178) although cost and quantity are known
- KEEP_OPERATIONS delete leaves a linked bank credit with kind ASSET_SELL (not income) although the sale record is gone
- Sell sheet has no date picker: a sale older than yesterday can only be dated by linking a bank credit (AssetTradeSheet.kt:194-198)
- Two parallel add flows (TradeMode.ADD with costKnown=null and zero lots vs AddHolding) produce assets with different rules; an asset with no lots shows value 'not available' in LoadHoldings.kt:41

### todo-ui
Lane: owner to-do items about unfinished screens (branch r051-int, read-only, 50 tool calls). T1 (several banks in onboarding) is not built: the whole chain from the card state to the use case carries exactly one bank. I found no item reported done that the code plainly does not do, but several have caveats listed in the T3 finding. Nothing in this lane produces wrong money.

### sec-firebase
Firebase/secrets lane, branch r051-int (0.5.5). The core isolation is sound: firestore.rules gives each account only its own users/{uid}/** tree, the feedback inbox is create-only with exact keys, no secret-named file (google-services.json, keystore, .env, service account) is tracked or was ever added in any ref, and the golden SMS data is synthetic. SpaceAdminSetup.kt decides no privilege in the client: it only wires the signed-in user's own users/{uid}/spaces/{id} paths, so the rules remain the only gate. Account deletion covers every collection under users/{uid} by construction (same constants as the repositories, awaited commits because the eraser builds a fresh FirestoreSpace with no writeScope); what stays behind is outside the user tree: inbox messages, sharedMerchants contributions (both by decision §80) and Crashlytics reports keyed by uid. The real risks are on the shared/abuse side: (1) the shared merchant catalog can be flooded or overwritten by any self-registered account, and every client then reads it without a limit and materialises each entry as a merchant in the user's own account; (2) open email sign-up + public config + App Check off + no size limits means one script can exhaust the free Spark quota for everyone (known as AUTH-1, still open); (3) CSV/file imports store the raw statement line and description unredacted, unlike the SMS path, which breaks the last-four-digits rule; (4) the price feed accepts any positive number with no plausibility bound. Secrets hygiene is good but the secret scan is manual only, and all distributed builds are signed with the per-machine debug key.

**Strengths:**
- Per-user isolation is one simple owner-only rule (firestore.rules:4-7) and the delete-account list is built from the same constants the repositories write with (native-app/firestore/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/firestore/UserCollections.kt:29-35), with a fresh FirestoreSpace so every delete commit is awaited (FirestoreAccountEraser.kt:19-25)
- SMS text is redacted before it becomes a stored row: phones, IBANs and long digit runs become dots + last four while amounts are kept (native-app/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/masroufy/core/BankSmsModel.kt:201-244); the golden SMS file in the public repo is synthetic (one all-zero IBAN, test store names)
- prices.yml is hardened: top-level permissions {}, contents: write only on the one job, actions pinned to commit hashes, persist-credentials false so the fetch scripts never see the token (.github/workflows/prices.yml:24-40,58-70)
- Feedback inbox is write-only with exact keys, server timestamp, size caps and no uid or email (firestore.rules:57-71 and FirestoreFeedbackInbox.kt:23-26)
- Secrets stay out of the public repo: google-services.json lives outside the tree and is git-ignored (.gitignore:54, androidApp/build.gradle.kts:5,171), no secret-named file was ever added in any ref, sign-out terminates and clears the local Firestore copy (androidApp/.../FirebaseSetup.kt:31-39), and password reset never reveals whether an email is registered (FirebaseAuthAdapter.kt:60-69)

**Not checked:**
- The rules actually live in the Firebase console (could not compare with firestore.rules; no deploy was run)
- Firebase console settings: enabled sign-in providers, email-enumeration protection, API key restrictions, App Check registration, usage alerts
- Whether a second device still signed in can recreate documents under users/{uid} after the account was erased (token stays valid for a while); not traced
- UserCollectionsGuardTest was not opened or run; coverage of the delete list is concluded from UserCollections.kt and from all writes going through FirestoreSpace.collection()
- scripts/fetch-prices.mjs and fetch-averages.mjs validation logic (only the source URLs were grepped)
- Whether asset price sync runs automatically or only on a user tap (SyncAssetPrices callers not traced)
- Contents of masroofi-claude-code.zip beyond the file name listing
- Local untracked git hooks on the owner's machine that might run secretscan
- AccountSession.kt sign-out/listener teardown and user-switch behaviour (only AndroidSignOutCleanup and wipeLocalCopy were read)
- DeviceSignOut.wipe contents (which device files are cleared at sign-out) and whether the support outbox and problem log files are cleared
- The private-repo job that reads and deletes inbox messages with a service account
- Assistant collections: what user free text is stored (only maskLongDigits on unknown questions was seen in AssistStorage.kt:209-210)
- iOS paths (built only, never run per the code comments)
- firestore.indexes.json and the emulator-only rules files beyond their match/allow lines (test.rules is open 'if true' but is referenced only by emulator configs)
- Whether real bank statement CSV rows used by testers actually contain full account numbers (inferred from .gitignore:12 comment, real files are not in the repo)

**Other titles:**
- Email/password accounts are created without email verification and there is no account linking (FirebaseAuthAdapter.kt:50-58): someone can register with another person's email address
- Support message text (up to 2000 chars) is sent as typed with no long-digit masking (SendSupportMessage.kt:34); users may paste account numbers into inbox
- Shared merchant person-name protection relies only on the local people/transfer-party lists (core/SharedMerchants.kt:45-57): a shop named after a person not in the user's list is still uploaded
- users/{uid}/{document=**} has no type or size validation (firestore.rules:4-7): fine for privacy, but a modified client can store arbitrary data in its own space
- masroofi-claude-code.zip (110 KB binary) is tracked in the public repo; secretscan only checks newly added files so it was never inspected by the scan
- FIREBASE.md:46 publishes the web API key in the public repo (not secret by itself, but it should be restricted by package/SHA in the console)
- prices.yml pushes to HEAD:GITHUB_REF_NAME (line 70): a manual run from another branch commits price files to that branch

### money
Money core is solid: amounts are Long minor units end to end, parsing is digit-based, splitting and inheritance allocation conserve every halala, and price x quantity uses 128-bit integer math with one rounding. No floating point was found in ui/data/firestore/wiring money paths (ui Float hits are drawing weights and animation only). The one serious problem is typed and CSV amounts: parseMoney deletes every comma without checking grouping, so "12,50" is saved as 1,250.00 (the SMS reader was already fixed for this exact bug class, the shared parser was not).
